    <div id="toastBar"></div>

    <script src="assets/js/sidebar.js"></script>
    <script src="assets/js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
    (function () {
        'use strict';

        const API_BASE = API_URL;
        const FEATURES = [
            { key: 'dashboard', icon: '📊', label: 'Dashboard', desc: 'Visão geral com monitoramento em tempo real' },
            { key: 'clients', icon: '👥', label: 'Clientes', desc: 'Gerenciar clientes, importação e mensagens' },
            { key: 'products', icon: '📦', label: 'Produtos', desc: 'Produtos, categorias, planos e recursos' },
            { key: 'whatsapp', icon: '📱', label: 'Integração WhatsApp', desc: 'Configuração e status do WhatsApp' },
            { key: 'telegram', icon: '✈️', label: 'Integração Telegram', desc: 'Configuração do bot do Telegram' },
            { key: 'settings', icon: '⚙️', label: 'Configurações', desc: 'Notificações e preferências pessoais' },
            { key: 'resell', icon: '💼', label: 'Revendedores', desc: 'Gerenciar revendedores e subusuários' },
            { key: 'admin', icon: '👑', label: 'Admin', desc: 'Console exclusivo do super admin' }
        ];

        let allUsers = [];
        let selectedUser = null;

        const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchUser');
        const userSelect = document.getElementById('userSelect');
        const profileBar = document.getElementById('userProfileBar');
        const panelUser = document.getElementById('panelUser');
        const panelReseller = document.getElementById('panelReseller');
        const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
        const flagsBody = document.getElementById('flagsBody');
        const resellerBody = document.getElementById('resellerFlagsBody');

        function toast(message, type = 'info') {
            const bar = document.getElementById('toastBar');
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = `toast-msg ${type}`;
            el.textContent = message;
            bar.appendChild(el);
            setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3200);
        }

        function authHeaders() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
                return {};
            }
            return { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };
        }

        function renderToggles(container, flags, idPrefix, disableAdmin) {
            container.innerHTML = FEATURES.map((feature) => {
                const enabled = flags[feature.key] !== false;
                const locked = disableAdmin && feature.key === 'admin';
                const inputId = `${idPrefix}_${feature.key}`;
                return `
                    <div class="flag-item">
                        <div class="flag-item-info">
                            <div class="flag-item-icon">${feature.icon}</div>
                            <div class="flag-item-text">
                                <strong>${feature.label}</strong>
                                <span>${feature.desc}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <label class="toggle-switch">
                            <input type="checkbox" id="${inputId}" data-feature="${feature.key}" ${enabled ? 'checked' : ''} ${locked ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                            <span class="toggle-slider"></span>
                        </label>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }

        function collectFlags(container) {
            const result = {};
            container.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]').forEach((input) => {
                result[input.getAttribute('data-feature')] = input.checked;
            });
            return result;
        }

        function populateSelect(users) {
            userSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">— selecione —</option>';
            users.forEach((user) => {
                const option = document.createElement('option');
                option.value = user.id;
                option.textContent = `${user.name} (${user.email}) — ${user.role}`;
                option.dataset.user = JSON.stringify(user);
                userSelect.appendChild(option);
            });
        }

        function showPanels(user) {
            emptyState.style.display = 'none';
            panelUser.style.display = '';
            profileBar.style.display = 'flex';

            document.getElementById('profileName').textContent = user.name;
            document.getElementById('profileEmail').textContent = user.email;
            document.getElementById('profileId').textContent = user.id;

            const badge = document.getElementById('profileRoleBadge');
            badge.textContent = user.role === 'super_admin' ? 'Super Admin' : user.role === 'reseller' ? 'Revendedor' : 'Usuário';
            badge.className = `role-badge ${user.role}`;

            renderToggles(flagsBody, user.feature_flags || {}, 'usr', user.role === 'super_admin');

            if (user.role === 'reseller') {
                panelReseller.style.display = '';
                renderToggles(resellerBody, user.reseller_feature_flags || {}, 'res', true);
            } else {
                panelReseller.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }

        function hidePanels() {
            emptyState.style.display = '';
            panelUser.style.display = 'none';
            panelReseller.style.display = 'none';
            profileBar.style.display = 'none';
        }

        async function loadUsers() {
            toast('Carregando usuários...');
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/users`, { headers: authHeaders() });
                if (response.status === 403) {
                    toast('Acesso negado. Apenas Super Admin.', 'error');
                    return;
                }
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error(await response.text());
                allUsers = await response.json();
                populateSelect(allUsers);
                hidePanels();
                toast(`${allUsers.length} usuário(s) carregado(s).`, 'success');
            } catch (error) {
                toast(`Erro ao carregar usuários: ${error.message}`, 'error');
            }
        }

        searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
            const query = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const filtered = query
                ? allUsers.filter((user) => user.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || user.email.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                : allUsers;
            populateSelect(filtered);
            hidePanels();
        });

        userSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
            const option = userSelect.selectedOptions[0];
            if (!option || !option.value) {
                hidePanels();
                return;
            }
            selectedUser = JSON.parse(option.dataset.user);
            showPanels(selectedUser);
        });

        document.getElementById('btnRefresh').addEventListener('click', loadUsers);
        document.getElementById('btnEnableAll').addEventListener('click', () => {
            flagsBody.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]:not(:disabled)').forEach((input) => { input.checked = true; });
        });
        document.getElementById('btnDisableAll').addEventListener('click', () => {
            flagsBody.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]:not(:disabled)').forEach((input) => { input.checked = false; });
        });
        document.getElementById('btnEnableAllReseller').addEventListener('click', () => {
            resellerBody.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]:not(:disabled)').forEach((input) => { input.checked = true; });
        });
        document.getElementById('btnDisableAllReseller').addEventListener('click', () => {
            resellerBody.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]:not(:disabled)').forEach((input) => { input.checked = false; });
        });

        document.getElementById('btnApply').addEventListener('click', async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (!selectedUser) {
                toast('Selecione um usuário primeiro.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            const flags = collectFlags(flagsBody);
            if (selectedUser.role === 'super_admin') flags.admin = true;

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/feature-flags/${selectedUser.id}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: authHeaders(),
                    body: JSON.stringify({ feature_flags: flags })
                });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error(await response.text());
                const saved = await response.json();
                const savedFlags = saved.feature_flags || flags;

                const myId = Number(localStorage.getItem('user_id'));
                if (Number(selectedUser.id) === myId) {
                    localStorage.setItem('user_feature_flags', JSON.stringify(savedFlags));
                    if (window.applyFeatureFlags) window.applyFeatureFlags();
                }

                toast('Permissões salvas com sucesso!', 'success');
                await loadUsers();
                const refreshed = allUsers.find((user) => Number(user.id) === Number(selectedUser.id));
                if (refreshed) {
                    selectedUser = refreshed;
                    userSelect.value = String(selectedUser.id);
                    showPanels(selectedUser);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                toast(`Erro ao salvar: ${error.message}`, 'error');
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('btnApplyReseller').addEventListener('click', async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (!selectedUser || selectedUser.role !== 'reseller') {
                toast('Somente para revendedores.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            const flags = collectFlags(resellerBody);

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/admin/reseller-feature-flags/${selectedUser.id}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: authHeaders(),
                    body: JSON.stringify({ reseller_feature_flags: flags })
                });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error(await response.text());
                toast('Padrão do revendedor salvo!', 'success');
                await loadUsers();
                const refreshed = allUsers.find((user) => Number(user.id) === Number(selectedUser.id));
                if (refreshed) {
                    selectedUser = refreshed;
                    userSelect.value = String(selectedUser.id);
                    showPanels(selectedUser);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                toast(`Erro ao salvar: ${error.message}`, 'error');
            }
        });

        async function checkAccess() {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
                return;
            }

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/users/me`, {
                    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
                });
                if (!response.ok) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                const me = await response.json();
                if (me.role !== 'super_admin') {
                    document.querySelector('.main-content').innerHTML = `
                        <section class="content-section">
                            <div style="text-align:center; padding:4rem 1rem;">
                                <div style="font-size:4rem; margin-bottom:1rem;">🚫</div>
                                <h2 style="margin-bottom:.5rem;">Acesso Restrito</h2>
                                <p style="color:var(--text-muted);">Esta área é exclusiva para Super Administradores.</p>
                                <a href="dashboard.html" class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top:1.5rem;">← Voltar ao Dashboard</a>
                            </div>
                        </section>
                    `;
                    return;
                }
                document.querySelectorAll('#userName').forEach((element) => { element.textContent = me.name || me.email; });
                await loadUsers();
            } catch (error) {
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', checkAccess);
    })();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </main>
    </div>

    <div id="connectionTestModal" style="display:none; position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(15,23,42,0.7); z-index:2000; align-items:center; justify-content:center; padding:20px;">
        <div class="card payment-test-modal-card">
            <button type="button" onclick="closeConnectionTestModal()" style="position:absolute; top:14px; right:14px; border:none; background:none; font-size:1.2rem; cursor:pointer;">✖</button>
            <h3 id="connectionTestTitle" style="margin-bottom:1rem; color:var(--primary);">Teste de conexão</h3>
            <p id="connectionTestDescription" style="margin-bottom:1rem; color:var(--text-muted);">Preencha com um produto fictício e um valor para validar a comunicação com o gateway.</p>
            <div class="form-group">
                <label>Nome do produto fictício</label>
                <input type="text" id="testProductName" placeholder="Ex: Produto Teste Nexus">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
                <label>Valor</label>
                <input type="number" id="testProductValue" min="1.00" step="0.01" placeholder="Ex: 10.00" value="10.00">
            </div>
            <div id="pagbankTestFields" style="display:none;">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>E-mail do comprador</label>
                    <input type="email" id="pagbankTestBuyerEmail" placeholder="Use um e-mail diferente da conta PagBank">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>CPF do comprador</label>
                    <input type="text" id="pagbankTestCpf" placeholder="Ex: 12345678901">
                </div>
                <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:120px 1fr; gap:10px;">
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label>DDD</label>
                        <input type="text" id="pagbankTestPhoneArea" placeholder="11">
                    </div>
                    <div class="form-group">
                        <label>Telefone</label>
                        <input type="text" id="pagbankTestPhoneNumber" placeholder="999999999">
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="form-group payment-test-result" id="connectionTestResult" style="display:none; padding:12px; border-radius:8px; background:#0f172a; color:#e2e8f0; white-space:pre-wrap;"></div>
            <div class="payment-modal-actions" style="margin-top:1.25rem;">
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeConnectionTestModal()">Cancelar</button>
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="runConnectionTest(false)">Testar conexão</button>
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" id="fullConnectionTestButton" onclick="runConnectionTest(true)">Teste completo</button>
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-success" id="realCheckoutButton" onclick="generateRealCheckout()" style="background-color:#10b981;">Gerar checkout real</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="assets/js/sidebar.js"></script>
    <script src="assets/js/main.js"></script>
    <script>
        const DEFAULT_PAYMENT_SETTINGS = {
            gateway_name: 'InfinitePay Checkout',
            bank_name: '',
            handle: '',
            api_base_url: 'https://api.checkout.infinitepay.io',
            links_endpoint: '/links',
            payment_check_endpoint: '/payment_check',
            webhook_url: '',
            redirect_url: '',
            notes: '',
            pagbank_environment: 'production',
            pagbank_access_token: '',
            pagbank_webhook_url: '',
            mercadopago_checkout_mode: 'production',
            mercadopago_access_token: '',
            mercadopago_public_key: '',
            mercadopago_webhook_url: '',
            mercadopago_success_url: '',
            mercadopago_pending_url: '',
            mercadopago_failure_url: '',
            mercadopago_statement_descriptor: '',
        };

        const GATEWAY_FIELDS = {
            infinitepay: {
                gateway_name: 'gatewayName',
                bank_name: 'bankName',
                handle: 'handle',
                api_base_url: 'apiBaseUrl',
                links_endpoint: 'linksEndpoint',
                payment_check_endpoint: 'paymentCheckEndpoint',
                webhook_url: 'webhookUrl',
                redirect_url: 'redirectUrl',
                notes: 'notes'
            },
            pagbank: {
                pagbank_environment: 'pagbankEnvironment',
                pagbank_access_token: 'pagbankAccessToken',
                pagbank_webhook_url: 'pagbankWebhookUrl',
            },
            mercadopago: {
                mercadopago_checkout_mode: 'mercadopagoCheckoutMode',
                mercadopago_access_token: 'mercadopagoAccessToken',
                mercadopago_public_key: 'mercadopagoPublicKey',
                mercadopago_webhook_url: 'mercadopagoWebhookUrl',
                mercadopago_success_url: 'mercadopagoSuccessUrl',
                mercadopago_pending_url: 'mercadopagoPendingUrl',
                mercadopago_failure_url: 'mercadopagoFailureUrl',
                mercadopago_statement_descriptor: 'mercadopagoStatementDescriptor',
            }
        };

        let currentPaymentSettings = normalizePaymentSettings(DEFAULT_PAYMENT_SETTINGS);
        let currentAccountEmail = '';
        let currentConnectionTestGateway = 'infinitepay';

        function apiFetch(path, options = {}) {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
            const headers = Object.assign({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, options.headers || {});
            if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
            
            // Adiciona timeout de 10 segundos se não especificado
            const controller = new AbortController();
            const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000);
            
            return fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, Object.assign({}, options, { headers, signal: controller.signal }))
                .finally(() => clearTimeout(timeoutId));
        }

        function getNotificationContainer() {
            return document.getElementById('notificationContainer') || (() => {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.id = 'notificationContainer';
                div.style.position = 'fixed';
                div.style.top = '20px';
                div.style.right = '20px';
                div.style.zIndex = '1000';
                div.style.display = 'flex';
                div.style.flexDirection = 'column';
                div.style.gap = '10px';
                document.body.appendChild(div);
                return div;
            })();
        }

        function createNotification(message, type = 'info') {
            const container = getNotificationContainer();

            const note = document.createElement('div');
            note.className = `notification ${type}`;
            note.textContent = message;
            note.style.padding = '10px 16px';
            note.style.borderRadius = '6px';
            note.style.color = '#fff';
            note.style.fontWeight = '600';
            note.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)';
            note.style.background = type === 'success' ? '#16a34a' : (type === 'error' ? '#dc2626' : '#2563eb');
            container.appendChild(note);
            return note;
        }

        function showNotification(message, type = 'info') {
            const note = createNotification(message, type);
            setTimeout(() => note.remove(), 4000);
        }

        function showPersistentNotification(message, type = 'info') {
            return createNotification(message, type);
        }

        function setSaveButtonLoading(gatewayKey, isLoading) {
            const selector = gatewayKey
                ? `button[data-save-gateway="${gatewayKey}"]`
                : 'button[data-save-gateway]';
            document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach((button) => {
                if (!button.dataset.defaultLabel) {
                    button.dataset.defaultLabel = button.textContent.trim();
                }
                button.disabled = isLoading;
                button.textContent = isLoading ? 'Salvando...' : button.dataset.defaultLabel;
            });
        }

        function logout() {
            if (confirm('Tem certeza que deseja sair?')) {
                localStorage.removeItem('token');
                localStorage.removeItem('user_role');
                localStorage.removeItem('user_feature_flags');
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
            }
        }

        function normalizePaymentSettings(saved) {
            return Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_PAYMENT_SETTINGS, saved || {});
        }

        function setGatewayPanelState(panel, shouldOpen) {
            if (!panel) {
                return;
            }

            const content = panel.querySelector('.gateway-panel-content');
            const toggle = panel.querySelector('.gateway-panel-toggle');
            if (!content || !toggle) {
                return;
            }

            panel.classList.toggle('is-open', shouldOpen);
            toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', shouldOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
            content.style.maxHeight = shouldOpen ? `${content.scrollHeight}px` : '0px';
        }

        function toggleGatewayPanel(panelId) {
            const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
            if (!panel) {
                return;
            }
            setGatewayPanelState(panel, !panel.classList.contains('is-open'));
        }

        function refreshGatewayPanels() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.gateway-panel').forEach((panel) => {
                setGatewayPanelState(panel, panel.classList.contains('is-open'));
            });
        }

        function fillGatewaySettings(settings, gatewayKey) {
            const fieldMap = GATEWAY_FIELDS[gatewayKey];
            Object.entries(fieldMap).forEach(([settingKey, elementId]) => {
                const field = document.getElementById(elementId);
                if (field) {
                    field.value = settings[settingKey] || '';
                }
            });
        }

        function fillPaymentSettings(settings, gatewayKey = null) {
            if (!gatewayKey || gatewayKey === 'infinitepay') {
                fillGatewaySettings(settings, 'infinitepay');
            }
            if (!gatewayKey || gatewayKey === 'pagbank') {
                fillGatewaySettings(settings, 'pagbank');
            }
            if (!gatewayKey || gatewayKey === 'mercadopago') {
                fillGatewaySettings(settings, 'mercadopago');
            }
            requestAnimationFrame(refreshGatewayPanels);
        }

        function loadPaymentSettings(gatewayKey = null) {
            console.log('Iniciando carregamento de configurações...');
            apiFetch('/users/me')
                .then((response) => {
                    console.log('Resposta de /users/me:', response.status);
                    if (response.status === 401) {
                        window.location.href = 'login.html';
                        return null;
                    }
                    if (!response.ok) {
                        throw new Error('Falha ao carregar configurações.');
                    }
                    return response.json();
                })
                .then((user) => {
                    if (!user) return;
                    console.log('Usuário carregado:', user.name);
                    document.getElementById('userName').textContent = user.name || 'Usuário';
                    currentAccountEmail = String(user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
                    currentPaymentSettings = normalizePaymentSettings(user.payment_api_settings);
                    fillPaymentSettings(currentPaymentSettings, gatewayKey);
                    console.log('Configurações preenchidas');
                })
                .catch((error) => {
                    console.error('Erro ao carregar:', error);
                    showNotification('Não foi possível carregar a configuração salva.', 'error');
                    currentAccountEmail = '';
                    currentPaymentSettings = normalizePaymentSettings(DEFAULT_PAYMENT_SETTINGS);
                    fillPaymentSettings(currentPaymentSettings, gatewayKey);
                });
        }

        function readGatewayFormPaymentSettings(gatewayKey) {
            const fieldMap = GATEWAY_FIELDS[gatewayKey];
            return Object.entries(fieldMap).reduce((result, [settingKey, elementId]) => {
                const field = document.getElementById(elementId);
                result[settingKey] = field ? field.value.trim() : '';
                return result;
            }, {});
        }

        function readFormPaymentSettings() {
            return normalizePaymentSettings(Object.assign(
                {},
                readGatewayFormPaymentSettings('infinitepay'),
                readGatewayFormPaymentSettings('pagbank'),
                readGatewayFormPaymentSettings('mercadopago')
            ));
        }

        function mergePaymentSettings(baseSettings, updatedSettings) {
            return normalizePaymentSettings(Object.assign({}, baseSettings || {}, updatedSettings || {}));
        }

        async function verifySavedSettings(expected, gatewayKey = null) {
            const response = await apiFetch('/users/me');
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('Não foi possível validar o salvamento.');
            }
            const user = await response.json();
            const saved = normalizePaymentSettings(user.payment_api_settings);
            const keys = gatewayKey ? Object.keys(GATEWAY_FIELDS[gatewayKey]) : Object.keys(expected);
            const mismatches = keys.filter((key) => {
                if (typeof expected[key] === 'boolean') {
                    return Boolean(saved[key]) !== Boolean(expected[key]);
                }
                return String(saved[key] || '') !== String(expected[key] || '');
            });
            return { user, mismatches };
        }

        function resetPaymentSettings(gatewayKey = null) {
            fillPaymentSettings(DEFAULT_PAYMENT_SETTINGS, gatewayKey);
        }

        function openConnectionTestModal(gatewayKey = 'infinitepay') {
            currentConnectionTestGateway = gatewayKey;
            document.getElementById('connectionTestModal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('connectionTestResult').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('connectionTestResult').textContent = '';
            document.getElementById('testProductName').value = document.getElementById('testProductName').value || 'Produto Teste Nexus';
            document.getElementById('testProductValue').value = document.getElementById('testProductValue').value || '10.00';
            document.getElementById('connectionTestTitle').textContent = gatewayKey === 'pagbank'
                ? 'Teste de conexão do PagBank'
                : (gatewayKey === 'mercadopago' ? 'Teste de conexão do Mercado Pago' : 'Teste de conexão');
            document.getElementById('connectionTestDescription').textContent = gatewayKey === 'pagbank'
                ? 'Informe um produto fictício, valor, e-mail do comprador, CPF e telefone. O e-mail do comprador deve ser diferente do e-mail da conta PagBank.'
                : (gatewayKey === 'mercadopago'
                    ? 'Preencha um produto fictício e um valor para criar uma preference de Checkout Pro no Mercado Pago.'
                    : 'Preencha com um produto fictício e um valor para validar a comunicação com o gateway.');
            document.getElementById('pagbankTestFields').style.display = gatewayKey === 'pagbank' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('fullConnectionTestButton').style.display = (gatewayKey === 'pagbank' || gatewayKey === 'mercadopago') ? 'none' : '';
            document.getElementById('realCheckoutButton').style.display = gatewayKey === 'pagbank' ? 'none' : '';
        }

        function closeConnectionTestModal() {
            document.getElementById('connectionTestModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        async function runConnectionTest(fullTest = false) {
            const productName = document.getElementById('testProductName').value.trim();
            const value = parseFloat(String(document.getElementById('testProductValue').value).replace(',', '.'));
            const resultBox = document.getElementById('connectionTestResult');

            if (!productName) {
                showNotification('Informe o nome do produto fictício.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!value || value <= 0) {
                showNotification('Informe um valor válido (minimo R$ 1.00).', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (value < 1.00) {
                showNotification('O valor minimo é R$ 1.00.', 'error');
                return;
            }

            resultBox.style.display = 'block';
            resultBox.textContent = fullTest ? 'Executando teste completo...' : 'Testando conexão...';

            try {
                const body = {
                    product_name: productName,
                    amount: value
                };
                let endpoint = fullTest ? '/users/me/payment/test-complete' : '/users/me/payment/test-connection';

                if (currentConnectionTestGateway === 'pagbank') {
                    const buyerEmail = document.getElementById('pagbankTestBuyerEmail').value.trim();
                    const taxId = document.getElementById('pagbankTestCpf').value.trim();
                    const phoneArea = document.getElementById('pagbankTestPhoneArea').value.trim();
                    const phoneNumber = document.getElementById('pagbankTestPhoneNumber').value.trim();

                    if (!buyerEmail) {
                        showNotification('Informe um e-mail do comprador para testar o PagBank.', 'error');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (currentAccountEmail && buyerEmail.toLowerCase() === currentAccountEmail) {
                        showNotification('Use no teste um e-mail diferente do e-mail da conta PagBank.', 'error');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!taxId) {
                        showNotification('Informe o CPF para testar o PagBank.', 'error');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!phoneArea || !phoneNumber) {
                        showNotification('Informe DDD e telefone para testar o PagBank.', 'error');
                        return;
                    }

                    endpoint = '/users/me/payment/pagbank/test-connection';
                    body.buyer_email = buyerEmail;
                    body.tax_id = taxId;
                    body.phone_area = phoneArea;
                    body.phone_number = phoneNumber;
                    resultBox.textContent = 'Testando conexão com o PagBank...';
                } else if (currentConnectionTestGateway === 'mercadopago') {
                    endpoint = '/users/me/payment/mercadopago/test-connection';
                    resultBox.textContent = 'Criando preference de teste no Mercado Pago...';
                }

                const response = await apiFetch(endpoint, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify(body)
                });

                const data = await response.json();
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(data.detail || 'Falha ao testar a conexão.');
                }

                const checkoutText = data.checkout_response ? JSON.stringify(data.checkout_response, null, 2) : (data.gateway_response ? JSON.stringify(data.gateway_response, null, 2) : '');
                const webhookText = data.webhook_result ? JSON.stringify(data.webhook_result, null, 2) : '';
                const lines = [
                    data.message,
                    '',
                    `Cobrança gerada no gateway: ${data.checkout_created ? 'sim' : 'não'}`,
                    `Webhook recebido: ${data.webhook_received ? 'sim' : 'não'}`,
                    `Pagamento confirmado: ${data.payment_confirmed ? 'sim' : 'não'}`,
                ];
                if (data.order) {
                    lines.push(`Pedido salvo: ${data.order.id || 'n/d'}`);
                    lines.push(`Reference ID: ${data.order.reference_id || 'n/d'}`);
                    lines.push(`Status do gateway: ${data.order.status || 'n/d'}`);
                    if (data.order.payment_link) {
                        lines.push(`Link de pagamento: ${data.order.payment_link}`);
                    }
                    if (data.order.sandbox_payment_link) {
                        lines.push(`Link sandbox: ${data.order.sandbox_payment_link}`);
                    }
                    if (data.order.qr_code_text) {
                        lines.push(`Pix copia e cola: ${data.order.qr_code_text}`);
                    }
                }
                if (checkoutText) {
                    lines.push('');
                    lines.push('Resposta da cobrança:');
                    lines.push(checkoutText);
                }
                if (webhookText) {
                    lines.push('');
                    lines.push('Resposta do webhook:');
                    lines.push(webhookText);
                }
                lines.push('');
                lines.push('sucesso!');
                resultBox.textContent = lines.join('\n');
                showNotification(fullTest ? 'Teste completo concluído com sucesso!' : 'Teste de conexão concluído com sucesso!', 'success');
            } catch (error) {
                resultBox.textContent = `Erro: ${error.message}`;
                showNotification(error.message || 'Erro no teste de conexão.', 'error');
            }
        }

        async function generateRealCheckout() {
            const productName = document.getElementById('testProductName').value.trim();
            const value = parseFloat(String(document.getElementById('testProductValue').value).replace(',', '.'));
            const resultBox = document.getElementById('connectionTestResult');

            if (!productName) {
                showNotification('Informe o nome do produto fictício.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!value || value <= 0) {
                showNotification('Informe um valor válido (minimo R$ 1.00).', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (value < 1.00) {
                showNotification('O valor minimo é R$ 1.00.', 'error');
                return;
            }

            resultBox.style.display = 'block';
            resultBox.textContent = 'Gerando checkout real no gateway...\n\nAguarde...';

            try {
                const endpoint = currentConnectionTestGateway === 'mercadopago'
                    ? '/users/me/payment/mercadopago/create-checkout'
                    : '/users/me/payment/create-checkout';
                const response = await apiFetch(endpoint, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        product_name: productName,
                        amount: value
                    })
                });

                const data = await response.json();
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(data.detail || 'Falha ao gerar checkout.');
                }

                const checkoutUrl = data.checkout_url || data.url;
                if (!checkoutUrl) {
                    throw new Error('Gateway nao retornou uma URL de checkout valida.');
                }

                const isMercadoPago = currentConnectionTestGateway === 'mercadopago';
                const message = isMercadoPago
                    ? 'Checkout Mercado Pago gerado com sucesso!\n\nVoce sera redirecionado para o gateway.\n\nO webhook sera recebido em /v1/webhooks/mercadopago quando a conta estiver configurada com URL publica.'
                    : 'Checkout gerado com sucesso!\n\nVoce sera redirecionado para o gateway...\n\nApos completar o pagamento, retorne a esta pagina e clique em "Sincronizar pagamento" para confirmar a renovacao.';
                const extraLines = [message, '', 'URL: ' + checkoutUrl];
                if (isMercadoPago && data.sandbox_checkout_url) {
                    extraLines.push('URL sandbox: ' + data.sandbox_checkout_url);
                }
                resultBox.textContent = extraLines.join('\n');
                showNotification(isMercadoPago ? 'Redirecionando para o Mercado Pago em 3 segundos...' : 'Redirecionando para o gateway em 3 segundos...', 'success');

                // Abre o checkout em nova aba
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.open(checkoutUrl, '_blank');
                }, 1500);

                // Fecha o modal automaticamente apos 5 segundos
                setTimeout(() => {
                    closeConnectionTestModal();
                }, 5000);

            } catch (error) {
                resultBox.textContent = `Erro: ${error.message}`;
                showNotification(error.message || 'Erro ao gerar checkout.', 'error');
            }
        }

        async function syncPayment() {
            try {
                showNotification('Sincronizando pagamento...', 'info');
                const response = await apiFetch('/users/me/payment/sync', {
                    method: 'POST'
                });

                const data = await response.json();
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error(data.detail || data.message || 'Falha ao sincronizar.');
                }

                if (data.success) {
                    showNotification(data.message, 'success');
                    setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 2000);
                } else {
                    showNotification(data.message || 'Pagamento ainda nao confirmado.', 'warning');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showNotification(error.message || 'Erro ao sincronizar pagamento.', 'error');
            }
        }

        function savePaymentSettings(gatewayKey = null) {
            const data = gatewayKey
                ? mergePaymentSettings(currentPaymentSettings, readGatewayFormPaymentSettings(gatewayKey))
                : readFormPaymentSettings();
            const loadingNote = showPersistentNotification('Salvando configuração...', 'info');
            setSaveButtonLoading(gatewayKey, true);

            apiFetch('/users/me/settings', {
                method: 'PATCH',
                body: JSON.stringify({ payment_api_settings: data })
            })
            .then((response) => {
                if (response.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return null;
                }
                if (!response.ok) {
                    return response.json().then((err) => {
                        throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao salvar.');
                    });
                }
                return response.json();
            })
            .then((user) => {
                if (!user) return;
                currentPaymentSettings = normalizePaymentSettings(user.payment_api_settings);
                fillPaymentSettings(currentPaymentSettings, gatewayKey);
                return verifySavedSettings(data, gatewayKey);
            })
            .then((result) => {
                if (!result) return;
                if (result.mismatches.length) {
                    showNotification('Salvo com diferença em alguns campos. Recarregue a página.', 'error');
                    console.warn('Diferenças após salvamento:', result.mismatches);
                    return;
                }
                showNotification('Configuração salva e validada na sua conta.', 'success');
            })
            .catch((error) => {
                showNotification(error.message || 'Erro ao salvar configuração.', 'error');
            })
            .finally(() => {
                loadingNote.remove();
                setSaveButtonLoading(gatewayKey, false);
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadPaymentSettings();
            refreshGatewayPanels();
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', refreshGatewayPanels);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                                <h3 id="formTitle" class="mb-1">Nova categoria</h3>
                                <p class="text-muted mb-0">Crie grupos para organizar e localizar produtos com mais facilidade.</p>

                            <input type="hidden" id="categoryId">

                                        <input type="text" id="categoryName" class="form-control" placeholder="Ex: Categoria Premium">
                                    </div>

    <script>
        function resetForm() {
            document.getElementById('categoryId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('categoryName').value = '';
            document.getElementById('categoryDescription').value = '';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Nova categoria';
        }

        function editCategory(category) {
            document.getElementById('categoryId').value = category.id;
            document.getElementById('categoryName').value = category.name || '';
            document.getElementById('categoryDescription').value = category.description || '';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Editar categoria';
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        function editCategoryFromEncoded(encoded) {
            editCategory(decodeItem(encoded));
        }

        async function loadCategories() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('categoriesTableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted">Carregando...</td></tr>';
            try {
                const [categoriesResp, productsResp] = await Promise.all([apiFetch('/categories'), apiFetch('/products')]);
                if (categoriesResp.status === 401 || productsResp.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                if (!categoriesResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar categorias.');
                if (!productsResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar produtos.');
                const [items, products] = await Promise.all([categoriesResp.json(), productsResp.json()]);
                const usageMap = {};
                products.forEach((product) => {
                    if (product.category_id) usageMap[product.category_id] = (usageMap[product.category_id] || 0) + 1;
                });
                document.getElementById('categoryCount').textContent = `${items.length} item(ns)`;
                if (!items.length) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted">Nenhuma categoria cadastrada.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }
                tbody.innerHTML = items.map((item) => `
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></td>
                        <td>${usageMap[item.id] ? `<span class="badge bg-primary">${usageMap[item.id]} produto(s)</span>` : '<span class="text-muted">Livre</span>'}</td>
                        <td>${item.description ? escapeHtml(item.description) : '<span class="text-muted">Sem descrição</span>'}</td>
                        <td>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick="editCategoryFromEncoded('${encodeItem(item)}')">Editar</button>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="deleteCategory(${item.id})">Excluir</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');
            } catch (error) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-danger">Erro ao carregar categorias.</td></tr>';
            }
        }

        async function saveCategory() {
            const id = document.getElementById('categoryId').value;
            const payload = {
                name: document.getElementById('categoryName').value.trim(),
                description: document.getElementById('categoryDescription').value.trim()
            };
            if (!payload.name) {
                notify('Informe o nome da categoria.', 'error');
                return;
            }

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(id ? `/categories/${id}` : '/categories', {
                    method: id ? 'PUT' : 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                if (!response.ok) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao salvar categoria.');
                }
                notify(id ? 'Categoria atualizada com sucesso.' : 'Categoria criada com sucesso.', 'success');
                resetForm();
                loadCategories();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        async function deleteCategory(id) {
            const row = document.querySelector(`button[onclick="deleteCategory(${id})"]`);
            const usageCell = row ? row.closest('tr').children[1] : null;
            if (usageCell && usageCell.textContent.indexOf('produto') !== -1) {
                notify('Remova os produtos vinculados antes de excluir esta categoria.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!confirm('Deseja excluir esta categoria?')) return;
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(`/categories/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (!response.ok && response.status !== 204) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao excluir categoria.');
                }
                notify('Categoria excluída.', 'success');
                loadCategories();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadCategories);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

============================================================
FILE: frontend\cleanup.js
============================================================
import { getInstances, removeInstance } from "./instanceManager.js";

export function startCleanup() {
  setInterval(() => {
    const now = Date.now();

    getInstances().forEach(inst => {
      if (now - inst.lastSeen > 60000) {
        console.log("🧹 Removendo instância órfã:", inst.name);
        removeInstance(inst.name);
      }
    });
  }, 30000);
}

    <!-- MODAL DE ENVIO EM MASSA (NOVO) -->
    <div class="modal-overlay" id="batchModal">

            <span class="close-modal" onclick="closeBatchModal()">&times;</span>
            <h3 style="margin-bottom: 1rem; color: var(--primary);">🚀 Envio em Massa</h3>

                Selecione qual grupo de clientes deve receber a notificação agora:
