    <script src="assets/js/dashboard.js"></script>
    <script src="assets/js/sidebar.js"></script>

    <script>
        // ==========================================
        // LÓGICA DE CLIENTES E FILTROS
        // ==========================================

        let allClients = [];
        let filteredClients = [];
        let currentFilter = 'all';
        let currentPage = 1;
        let itemsPerPage = 20;
        let allPlans = [];
        let allProducts = [];
        const EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY = 'clients_expiration_range';
        const EXPIRATION_RANGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY = 'clients_expiration_range_inputs';
        let expirationRangeStart = '';
        let expirationRangeEnd = '';
        let expirationDaysAhead = '';
        const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [10, 20, 50, 100];
        let sortState = {
            key: null,
            direction: 'asc'
        };

        function normalizeFilterValue(value) {
            return (value || '')
                .toString()
                .normalize('NFD')
                .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                .trim()
                .toLowerCase();
        }

        function hasReminderError(client) {
            return Boolean(client && typeof client.reminder_error_message === 'string' && client.reminder_error_message.trim());
        }

        function escapeHtml(value) {
            return String(value ?? '')
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
        }

        function normalizeCurrencyInput(value) {
            const rawValue = String(value ?? '').trim();
            if (!rawValue) {
                return '';
            }

            if (rawValue.includes(',') && rawValue.includes('.')) {
                return rawValue.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.');
            }

            if (rawValue.includes(',')) {
                return rawValue.replace(',', '.');
            }

            return rawValue;
        }

        function parseCurrencyValue(value) {
            const normalizedValue = normalizeCurrencyInput(value);
            if (!normalizedValue) {
                return null;
            }

            const numericValue = Number.parseFloat(normalizedValue);
            return Number.isFinite(numericValue) ? numericValue : null;
        }

        function formatCurrencyInput(value) {
            const numericValue = parseCurrencyValue(value);
            if (numericValue === null) {
                return '';
            }
            return numericValue.toFixed(2);
        }

        function formatMoneyDisplay(value) {
            const numericValue = Number(value);
            if (!Number.isFinite(numericValue)) {
                return 'R$ 0,00';
            }
            return numericValue.toLocaleString('pt-BR', {
                style: 'currency',
                currency: 'BRL'
            });
        }

        function encodeClientData(client) {
            return encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(client));
        }

        function decodeClientData(encoded) {
            return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(encoded));
        }

        function isNotificationIntegrationMessage(message) {
            if (typeof message !== 'string') {
                return false;
            }
            return message.includes('WhatsApp não conectado. Configure na aba Integração.')
                || message.includes('Nenhum canal de notificação conectado.');
        }

        function showTopAlertBanner(message) {
            const banner = document.getElementById('topAlertBanner');
            const titleTarget = document.getElementById('topAlertBannerTitle');
            const messageTarget = document.getElementById('topAlertBannerMessage');
            if (!banner || !messageTarget || !titleTarget) {
                return;
            }
            const normalizedMessage = message || 'WhatsApp não conectado. Configure na aba Integração.';
            titleTarget.textContent = normalizedMessage.includes('Nenhum canal de notificação conectado.')
                ? 'Nenhum canal conectado'
                : 'WhatsApp desconectado';
            messageTarget.textContent = normalizedMessage;
            banner.classList.add('show');
        }

        function hideTopAlertBanner() {
            const banner = document.getElementById('topAlertBanner');
            if (!banner) {
                return;
            }
            banner.classList.remove('show');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            restoreExpirationRangeFilter();
            setupExpirationFilterInteractions();
            loadPlanOptions();
            loadProductCatalog();
            loadClients();
            loadScheduleStatus(); // Carrega o status do agendamento ao iniciar
            updateBatchRangeHint();
        });

        function getPlanNamesMatch(planName, selectedValue) {
            return String(planName || '').trim() === String(selectedValue || '').trim();
        }

        function getPlanByName(planName) {
            const normalizedPlanName = String(planName || '').trim();
            if (!normalizedPlanName) {
                return null;
            }
            return allPlans.find((plan) => getPlanNamesMatch(plan.name, normalizedPlanName)) || null;
        }

        function getAvailableProducts() {
            return Array.isArray(allProducts) ? allProducts : [];
        }

        function getProductById(productId) {
            if (productId === null || productId === undefined || productId === '') {
                return null;
            }
            return allProducts.find((product) => Number(product.id) === Number(productId)) || null;
        }

        function renderPlanOptions(selectedValue = '') {
            const select = document.getElementById('clientServer');
            if (!select) {
                return;
            }

            const normalizedSelectedValue = String(selectedValue || '').trim();
            const hasRegisteredPlans = Array.isArray(allPlans) && allPlans.length > 0;
            const hasLegacySelectedValue = normalizedSelectedValue
                && !allPlans.some((plan) => getPlanNamesMatch(plan.name, normalizedSelectedValue));

            const options = ['<option value="">Selecione um plano</option>'];

            if (hasLegacySelectedValue) {
                options.push(`<option value="${escapeHtml(normalizedSelectedValue)}">Plano atual: ${escapeHtml(normalizedSelectedValue)}</option>`);
            }

            if (hasRegisteredPlans) {
                allPlans.forEach((plan) => {
                    options.push(`<option value="${escapeHtml(plan.name)}">${escapeHtml(plan.name)}</option>`);
                });
            } else {
                options.push('<option value="" disabled>Nenhum plano cadastrado</option>');
            }

            select.innerHTML = options.join('');
            select.disabled = !hasRegisteredPlans && !hasLegacySelectedValue;
            select.value = normalizedSelectedValue;
        }

        async function loadPlanOptions(selectedValue = '') {
            const select = document.getElementById('clientServer');
            if (!select) {
                return;
            }

            select.disabled = true;
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Carregando planos...</option>';

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/plans');
                if (response.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Falha ao carregar planos.');
                }

                allPlans = await response.json();
                renderPlanOptions(selectedValue);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar planos:', error);
                allPlans = [];
                renderPlanOptions(selectedValue);
            }
        }

        async function loadProductCatalog() {
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/products');
                if (response.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                if (!response.ok) {
                    throw new Error('Falha ao carregar produtos.');
                }
                allProducts = await response.json();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar produtos:', error);
                allProducts = [];
            }
        }

        function syncPlanPriceWithSelection(forceUpdate = true) {
            const planSelect = document.getElementById('clientServer');
            const priceInput = document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice');
            if (!planSelect || !priceInput) {
                return;
            }

            const selectedPlan = getPlanByName(planSelect.value);
            if (!selectedPlan) {
                if (forceUpdate) {
                    priceInput.value = '';
                }
                return;
            }

            if (!forceUpdate && String(priceInput.value || '').trim()) {
                return;
            }

            priceInput.value = formatCurrencyInput(selectedPlan.price);
        }

        function updateProductsHint() {
            const hint = document.getElementById('clientProductsHint');
            if (!hint) {
                return;
            }
            const availableProducts = getAvailableProducts();
            hint.style.display = availableProducts.length === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
            hint.textContent = 'Nenhum produto cadastrado.';
        }

        function renderProductRowOptions(select, selectedItem = null) {
            if (!select) {
                return;
            }

            const availableProducts = getAvailableProducts();
            const selectedProductId = selectedItem && selectedItem.product_id != null ? String(selectedItem.product_id) : '';
            const selectedProductName = String(selectedItem?.name || '').trim();
            const hasLegacySelectedProduct = selectedProductName
                && !availableProducts.some((product) => String(product.id) === selectedProductId);

            const options = ['<option value="">Selecione um produto</option>'];

            if (hasLegacySelectedProduct) {
                options.push(`<option value="${escapeHtml(selectedProductId)}">Produto atual: ${escapeHtml(selectedProductName)}</option>`);
            }

            availableProducts.forEach((product) => {
                options.push(
                    `<option value="${product.id}">${escapeHtml(product.name)} - ${formatMoneyDisplay(product.price)}</option>`
                );
            });

            select.innerHTML = options.join('');
            select.value = selectedProductId;
            select.disabled = !availableProducts.length && !hasLegacySelectedProduct;
        }

        function syncProductRowPrice(row, fallbackItem = null) {
            if (!row) {
                return;
            }

            const select = row.querySelector('.client-product-select');
            const priceInput = row.querySelector('.client-product-price');
            if (!select || !priceInput) {
                return;
            }

            const selectedProduct = getProductById(select.value);
            if (selectedProduct) {
                priceInput.value = formatCurrencyInput(selectedProduct.price);
            } else if (fallbackItem && fallbackItem.price != null) {
                priceInput.value = formatCurrencyInput(fallbackItem.price);
            } else {
                priceInput.value = '';
            }
        }

        function updateClientTotalAmount() {
            const planValue = parseCurrencyValue(document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice')?.value) || 0;
            const productTotal = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.client-product-row .client-product-price'))
                .reduce((sum, input) => sum + (parseCurrencyValue(input.value) || 0), 0);
            const totalTarget = document.getElementById('clientTotalAmount');
            if (totalTarget) {
                totalTarget.textContent = formatMoneyDisplay(planValue + productTotal);
            }
        }

        function getClientTotalAmount(client) {
            const planValue = Number(client?.plan_price) || 0;
            const productTotal = Array.isArray(client?.selected_products)
                ? client.selected_products.reduce((sum, product) => sum + (Number(product?.price) || 0), 0)
                : 0;
            return planValue + productTotal;
        }

        async function addProductSelectionRow(selectedItem = null) {
            const container = document.getElementById('clientProductsContainer');
            if (!container) {
                return;
            }

            await loadProductCatalog();
            rerenderProductRows();

            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'client-product-row';
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:minmax(0, 1fr) 130px 96px; gap:0.75rem; align-items:center;">
                    <select class="client-product-select" style="width:100%; padding:10px; border:1px solid #ddd; border-radius:6px;" aria-label="Produto">
                        <option value="">Selecione um produto</option>
                    </select>
                    <input type="number" class="client-product-price" placeholder="0,00" min="0" step="0.01" aria-label="Valor do produto">
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="removeProductSelectionRow(this)" style="height:42px;">Remover</button>
                </div>
            `;

            container.appendChild(row);
            const select = row.querySelector('.client-product-select');
            renderProductRowOptions(select, selectedItem);
            syncProductRowPrice(row, selectedItem);
            updateProductsHint();
            updateClientTotalAmount();
        }

        function removeProductSelectionRow(button) {
            const row = button?.closest('.client-product-row');
            if (row) {
                row.remove();
            }
            updateProductsHint();
            updateClientTotalAmount();
        }

        function rerenderProductRows() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.client-product-row').forEach((row) => {
                const select = row.querySelector('.client-product-select');
                const priceInput = row.querySelector('.client-product-price');
                const existingItem = {
                    product_id: select && select.value ? Number(select.value) : null,
                    name: select && select.selectedOptions.length ? select.selectedOptions[0].textContent.replace(/\s-\sR\$.*/, '').replace(/^Produto atual:\s*/, '').trim() : '',
                    price: parseCurrencyValue(priceInput?.value) || 0
                };
                renderProductRowOptions(select, existingItem);
                syncProductRowPrice(row, existingItem);
            });
            updateProductsHint();
            updateClientTotalAmount();
        }

        function getSelectedProductsPayload() {
            return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.client-product-row')).map((row) => {
                const select = row.querySelector('.client-product-select');
                const priceInput = row.querySelector('.client-product-price');
                const selectedProduct = getProductById(select?.value);
                const selectedOptionText = select && select.selectedOptions.length ? select.selectedOptions[0].textContent : '';
                const fallbackName = selectedOptionText
                    .replace(/\s-\sR\$.*/, '')
                    .replace(/^Produto atual:\s*/, '')
                    .trim();
                const productName = selectedProduct ? selectedProduct.name : fallbackName;
                const productPrice = parseCurrencyValue(priceInput?.value);

                if (!productName || productPrice === null) {
                    return null;
                }

                return {
                    product_id: selectedProduct ? selectedProduct.id : null,
                    name: productName,
                    price: productPrice
                };
            }).filter(Boolean);
        }

        function validateSelectedProductsRows() {
            const rows = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.client-product-row'));
            for (const row of rows) {
                const select = row.querySelector('.client-product-select');
                const priceInput = row.querySelector('.client-product-price');
                const selectedProduct = getProductById(select?.value);
                const hasLegacyProduct = Boolean(
                    select && select.selectedOptions.length
                    && String(select.selectedOptions[0].textContent || '').trim()
                    && select.selectedOptions[0].textContent !== 'Selecione um produto'
                );
                const priceValue = parseCurrencyValue(priceInput?.value);

                if (!selectedProduct && !hasLegacyProduct) {
                    return 'Selecione um produto em todas as linhas adicionadas.';
                }

                if (priceValue === null) {
                    return 'Informe um valor válido para todos os produtos adicionados.';
                }
            }

            return null;
        }

        function setupExpirationFilterInteractions() {
            const startInput = document.getElementById('expirationStart');
            const endInput = document.getElementById('expirationEnd');
            const daysInput = document.getElementById('expirationDaysAhead');
            if (!startInput || !endInput || !daysInput) {
                return;
            }

            const persistCurrentInputs = () => {
                persistExpirationRangeInputs(startInput.value, endInput.value, daysInput.value.trim());
            };

            const clearDaysWhenDateIsFilled = () => {
                if (startInput.value) {
                    daysInput.value = '';
                }
                if (endInput.value) {
                    daysInput.value = '';
                }
                persistCurrentInputs();
            };

            startInput.addEventListener('input', clearDaysWhenDateIsFilled);
            startInput.addEventListener('change', clearDaysWhenDateIsFilled);

            endInput.addEventListener('input', clearDaysWhenDateIsFilled);
            endInput.addEventListener('change', clearDaysWhenDateIsFilled);

            daysInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                if (daysInput.value.trim()) {
                    startInput.value = '';
                    endInput.value = '';
                }
                persistCurrentInputs();
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
            if (event.target && event.target.id === 'clientServer') {
                syncPlanPriceWithSelection(true);
                rerenderProductRows();
            }
            if (event.target && event.target.classList.contains('client-product-select')) {
                const row = event.target.closest('.client-product-row');
                syncProductRowPrice(row);
                updateClientTotalAmount();
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('blur', (event) => {
            if (event.target && event.target.id === 'clientPlanPrice') {
                event.target.value = formatCurrencyInput(event.target.value);
                updateClientTotalAmount();
            }
            if (event.target && event.target.classList.contains('client-product-price')) {
                event.target.value = formatCurrencyInput(event.target.value);
                updateClientTotalAmount();
            }
        }, true);

        document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
            if (event.target && event.target.id === 'clientPlanPrice') {
                updateClientTotalAmount();
            }
            if (event.target && event.target.classList.contains('client-product-price')) {
                updateClientTotalAmount();
            }
        });

        // Alterna a exibição dos submenus da sidebar (comportamento sanfona)
        function toggleSubmenu(toggle) {
            const submenu = document.getElementById(toggle.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
            if (!submenu) return false;
            const willOpen = submenu.hidden;

            // Sanfona: fecha todos os outros submenus antes de abrir este
            if (willOpen) {
                document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-menu .submenu-toggle').forEach(function (other) {
                    if (other === toggle) return;
                    const otherSubmenu = document.getElementById(other.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
                    if (otherSubmenu) otherSubmenu.hidden = true;
                    other.classList.remove('expanded');
                    other.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
                });
            }

            submenu.hidden = !willOpen;
            toggle.classList.toggle('expanded', willOpen);
            toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(willOpen));
            return false;
        }

        function parseLocalDate(dateValue) {
            if (typeof dateValue !== 'string') {
                return null;
            }

            const value = dateValue.trim().slice(0, 10);
            if (!value) {
                return null;
            }

            const parts = value.split('-');
            if (parts.length !== 3) {
                return null;
            }

            const [year, month, day] = parts.map(Number);
            if (!year || !month || !day) {
                return null;
            }

            return new Date(year, month - 1, day);
        }

        function formatLocalDate(dateValue) {
            const parsedDate = parseLocalDate(dateValue);
            return parsedDate ? parsedDate.toLocaleDateString('pt-BR') : '';
        }

        function formatDateTime(value) {
            if (!value) {
                return 'nunca';
            }
            const parsed = new Date(value);
            if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
                return String(value);
            }
            return parsed.toLocaleString('pt-BR');
        }

        async function loadClients() {
            const list = document.getElementById('clientsList');
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/clients');
                const data = await response.json();

                const today = new Date();
                today.setHours(0,0,0,0);

                allClients = data.map(client => {
                    const expDate = parseLocalDate(client.expiration_date);
                    if (!expDate) {
                        return { ...client, diffDays: null };
                    }
                    expDate.setHours(0,0,0,0);
                    const diffTime = expDate - today;
                    const diffDays = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                    return { ...client, diffDays };
                });

                updateDashboardCounts();
                applyFilters();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                list.innerHTML = `<div style="color: red; padding: 1rem;">Erro ao carregar clientes.</div>`;
            }
        }

        function getSchedulerFilteredClients() {
            return allClients.filter(client => matchesExpirationRange(client.expiration_date, client.diffDays));
        }

        function updateDashboardCounts() {
            const schedulerFilteredClients = getSchedulerFilteredClients();
            const counts = { all: schedulerFilteredClients.length, expired: 0, d1: 0, d2: 0, d3: 0, d4: 0, errors: 0 };
            schedulerFilteredClients.forEach(c => {
                if (c.diffDays < 0) counts.expired++;
                if (c.diffDays === 1) counts.d1++;
                if (c.diffDays === 2) counts.d2++;
                if (c.diffDays === 3) counts.d3++;
                if (c.diffDays === 4) counts.d4++;
            });
            counts.errors = allClients.filter(hasReminderError).length;
            document.getElementById('count-all').textContent = counts.all;
            document.getElementById('count-expired').textContent = counts.expired;
            document.getElementById('count-1').textContent = counts.d1;
            document.getElementById('count-2').textContent = counts.d2;
            document.getElementById('count-3').textContent = counts.d3;
            document.getElementById('count-4').textContent = counts.d4;
            document.getElementById('count-errors').textContent = counts.errors;

            const errorCard = document.getElementById('card-errors');
            if (errorCard) {
                errorCard.classList.toggle('has-pending-errors', counts.errors > 0);
            }
        }

        function filterByStatus(status, cardElement) {
            currentFilter = status;
            document.querySelectorAll('.summary-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            if(cardElement) cardElement.classList.add('active');
            applyFilters();
        }

        function applyFilters(resetPage = true) {
            updateDashboardCounts();
            const searchTerm = normalizeFilterValue(document.getElementById('searchInput').value);
            let filtered = allClients.filter(client => {
                let statusMatch = false;
                if (currentFilter === 'all') statusMatch = true;
                else if (currentFilter === 'expired') statusMatch = client.diffDays < 0;
                else if (currentFilter === '1') statusMatch = client.diffDays === 1;
                else if (currentFilter === '2') statusMatch = client.diffDays === 2;
                else if (currentFilter === '3') statusMatch = client.diffDays === 3;
                else if (currentFilter === '4') statusMatch = client.diffDays === 4;
                else if (currentFilter === 'errors') statusMatch = hasReminderError(client);

                const clientName = normalizeFilterValue(client.name);
                const clientLogin = normalizeFilterValue(client.login);
                const clientWhatsapp = (client.whatsapp || '').toString().trim();
                const searchMatch =
                    !searchTerm ||
                    clientLogin.includes(searchTerm) ||
                    clientWhatsapp.includes(searchTerm) ||
                    clientName.includes(searchTerm);

                const expirationMatch = currentFilter === 'errors'
                    ? true
                    : matchesExpirationRange(client.expiration_date, client.diffDays);

                return statusMatch && searchMatch && expirationMatch;
            });

            filtered = sortClients(filtered);
            filteredClients = filtered;

            if (resetPage) {
                currentPage = 1;
            }

            const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredClients.length / itemsPerPage));
            if (currentPage > totalPages) {
                currentPage = totalPages;
            }

            renderTable(filteredClients);
        }

        function getExpirationDaysWindow(daysValue) {
            const parsedDays = Number.parseInt(daysValue, 10);
            if (!Number.isInteger(parsedDays) || parsedDays < 0) {
                return null;
            }

            const offset = Math.max(parsedDays - 1, 0);
            return {
                inputDays: parsedDays,
                minDiff: -offset,
                maxDiff: offset
            };
        }

        function matchesExpirationRange(expirationDate, diffDays) {
            if (expirationDaysAhead !== '') {
                const daysWindow = getExpirationDaysWindow(expirationDaysAhead);
                if (!daysWindow) {
                    return true;
                }
                return Number.isInteger(diffDays)
                    && diffDays >= daysWindow.minDiff
                    && diffDays <= daysWindow.maxDiff;
            }

            if (!expirationRangeStart && !expirationRangeEnd) {
                return true;
            }

            const value = expirationDate ? expirationDate.slice(0, 10) : '';
            if (!value) {
                return false;
            }

            if (expirationRangeStart && value < expirationRangeStart) {
                return false;
            }

            if (expirationRangeEnd && value > expirationRangeEnd) {
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        function validateExpirationRange(start, end, showMessage = true) {
            if (start && end && start > end) {
                if (showMessage) {
                    alert('A data inicial não pode ser maior que a data final.');
                }
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        function validateExpirationDays(daysValue, showMessage = true) {
            if (!daysValue) {
                return true;
            }

            const parsedDays = Number.parseInt(daysValue, 10);
            if (!Number.isInteger(parsedDays) || parsedDays < 0 || String(parsedDays) !== String(daysValue).trim()) {
                if (showMessage) {
                    alert('Informe uma quantidade de dias v\u00e1lida, usando apenas n\u00fameros inteiros iguais ou maiores que zero.');
                }
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        function setExpirationRange(start, end, daysAhead = '') {
            expirationRangeStart = start;
            expirationRangeEnd = end;
            expirationDaysAhead = daysAhead;
        }

        function getExpirationRangeInputs() {
            return {
                start: document.getElementById('expirationStart').value,
                end: document.getElementById('expirationEnd').value,
                daysAhead: document.getElementById('expirationDaysAhead').value.trim()
            };
        }

        function syncExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, daysAhead = '') {
            document.getElementById('expirationStart').value = start || '';
            document.getElementById('expirationEnd').value = end || '';
            document.getElementById('expirationDaysAhead').value = daysAhead || '';
        }

        function persistExpirationRange(start, end, daysAhead = '') {
            localStorage.setItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
                start: start || '',
                end: end || '',
                daysAhead: daysAhead || ''
            }));
        }

        function persistExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, daysAhead = '') {
            localStorage.setItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
                start: start || '',
                end: end || '',
                daysAhead: daysAhead || ''
            }));
        }

        function restoreExpirationRangeFilter() {
            const savedRange = localStorage.getItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY);
            const savedInputs = localStorage.getItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY);

            if (savedInputs) {
                try {
                    const parsedInputs = JSON.parse(savedInputs);
                    const inputStart = parsedInputs && typeof parsedInputs.start === 'string' ? parsedInputs.start : '';
                    const inputEnd = parsedInputs && typeof parsedInputs.end === 'string' ? parsedInputs.end : '';
                    const inputDaysAhead = parsedInputs && typeof parsedInputs.daysAhead === 'string' ? parsedInputs.daysAhead.trim() : '';

                    syncExpirationRangeInputs(inputStart, inputEnd, inputDaysAhead);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Erro ao restaurar campos de vencimento salvos.', error);
                    localStorage.removeItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY);
                }
            }

            if (!savedRange) {
                return;
            }

            try {
                const parsedRange = JSON.parse(savedRange);
                const start = parsedRange && typeof parsedRange.start === 'string' ? parsedRange.start : '';
                const end = parsedRange && typeof parsedRange.end === 'string' ? parsedRange.end : '';
                const daysAhead = parsedRange && typeof parsedRange.daysAhead === 'string' ? parsedRange.daysAhead.trim() : '';

                if (!validateExpirationRange(start, end, false) || !validateExpirationDays(daysAhead, false)) {
                    localStorage.removeItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY);
                    return;
                }

                if (daysAhead) {
                    setExpirationRange('', '', daysAhead);
                    syncExpirationRangeInputs('', '', daysAhead);
                    persistExpirationRangeInputs('', '', daysAhead);
                    return;
                }

                setExpirationRange(start, end, '');
                syncExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
                persistExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao restaurar filtro de vencimento salvo.', error);
                localStorage.removeItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY);
            }
        }

        function saveExpirationRangeFilter() {
            const { start, end, daysAhead } = getExpirationRangeInputs();
            if (!validateExpirationRange(start, end)) {
                return;
            }
            if (!validateExpirationDays(daysAhead)) {
                return;
            }

            if (start || end) {
                setExpirationRange(start, end, '');
                syncExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
                persistExpirationRange(start, end, '');
                persistExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
                applyFilters();
                updateBatchRangeHint();
                return;
            }

            if (daysAhead) {
                setExpirationRange('', '', daysAhead);
                syncExpirationRangeInputs('', '', daysAhead);
                persistExpirationRange('', '', daysAhead);
                persistExpirationRangeInputs('', '', daysAhead);
                applyFilters();
                updateBatchRangeHint();
                return;
            }

            setExpirationRange(start, end, '');
            syncExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
            persistExpirationRange(start, end, '');
            persistExpirationRangeInputs(start, end, '');
            applyFilters();
            updateBatchRangeHint();
        }

        function applyExpirationRangeFilter() {
            saveExpirationRangeFilter();
        }

        function clearExpirationRangeFilter() {
            setExpirationRange('', '', '');
            syncExpirationRangeInputs('', '', '');
            localStorage.removeItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_STORAGE_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(EXPIRATION_RANGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY);
            applyFilters();
            updateBatchRangeHint();
        }

        function toggleSort(key) {
            if (sortState.key === key) {
                sortState.direction = sortState.direction === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
            } else {
                sortState.key = key;
                sortState.direction = 'asc';
            }
            applyFilters(false);
        }

        function sortClients(clients) {
            if (!sortState.key) {
                return clients;
            }

            const direction = sortState.direction === 'desc' ? -1 : 1;
            return clients.slice().sort((a, b) => {
                let aValue;
                let bValue;

                if (sortState.key === 'expiration') {
                    aValue = parseLocalDate(a.expiration_date)?.getTime() || 0;
                    bValue = parseLocalDate(b.expiration_date)?.getTime() || 0;
                } else if (sortState.key === 'status') {
                    aValue = a.diffDays;
                    bValue = b.diffDays;
                } else {
                    return 0;
                }

                if (aValue < bValue) return -1 * direction;
                if (aValue > bValue) return 1 * direction;
                return 0;
            });
        }

        function renderTable(clients) {
            const list = document.getElementById('clientsList');
            if (clients.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = `<div class="table-empty-state">Nenhum cliente encontrado com estes filtros.</div>`;
                return;
            }

            const totalItems = clients.length;
            const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalItems / itemsPerPage));
            const safePage = Math.min(Math.max(currentPage, 1), totalPages);
            const startIndex = (safePage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
            const paginatedClients = clients.slice(startIndex, startIndex + itemsPerPage);
            const startItem = startIndex + 1;
            const endItem = Math.min(startIndex + itemsPerPage, totalItems);
            currentPage = safePage;

            let html = `
                <table class="clients-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>Nome / Obs</th>
                            <th>Login</th>
                            <th>WhatsApp</th>
                            <th>Valor</th>
                            <th class="sort-th">
                                Vencimento
                                <button type="button" class="sort-btn" title="Ordenar por vencimento" onclick="toggleSort('expiration')">
                                    ${sortState.key === 'expiration' ? (sortState.direction === 'asc' ? '↑' : '↓') : '↕'}
                                </button>
                            </th>
                            <th class="sort-th">
                                Status
                                <button type="button" class="sort-btn" title="Ordenar por status" onclick="toggleSort('status')">
                                    ${sortState.key === 'status' ? (sortState.direction === 'asc' ? '↑' : '↓') : '↕'}
                                </button>
                            </th>
                            <th>Ações</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
            `;

            html += paginatedClients.map(client => {
                let statusColor = 'var(--success)';
                let statusText = Number.isInteger(client.diffDays) ? `${client.diffDays} dias` : '--';
                if (client.diffDays < 0) { statusColor = 'var(--danger)'; statusText = 'Vencido'; }
                else if (client.diffDays === 0) { statusColor = 'var(--warning)'; statusText = 'Vence Hoje'; }
                else if (client.diffDays <= 3) { statusColor = 'var(--warning)'; }

                const notesHtml = client.notes ? `<span class="client-note-inline">📝 ${client.notes}</span>` : '';
                const errorHtml = hasReminderError(client)
                    ? `
                        <div class="client-error-inline" title="${escapeHtml(client.reminder_error_message)}">
                            <span class="client-error-icon">❗</span>
                            <span class="client-error-text">${escapeHtml(client.reminder_error_message)}</span>
                        </div>
                    `
                    : '';

                return `
                <tr>
                    <td data-label="Cliente" class="client-name-cell">
                        <div class="client-name-main">${client.name}</div>
                        ${notesHtml}
                        ${errorHtml}
                    </td>
                    <td data-label="Login">${client.login}</td>
                    <td data-label="WhatsApp">${client.whatsapp}</td>
                    <td data-label="Valor">${formatMoneyDisplay(getClientTotalAmount(client))}</td>
                    <td data-label="Vencimento">${formatLocalDate(client.expiration_date)}</td>
                    <td data-label="Status"><span class="status-badge" style="background: ${statusColor};">${statusText}</span></td>
                    <td data-label="Ações" class="client-actions-cell">
                        <div class="action-buttons">
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" title="Enviar Cobrança Agora" onclick="sendManualReminder(${client.id})">📨</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" title="Detalhes" onclick="openDetailsModalFromEncoded('${encodeClientData(client)}')">👁️</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" title="Editar" onclick="openClientModalFromEncoded('${encodeClientData(client)}')">✏️</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" title="Excluir" onclick="deleteClient(${client.id})">🗑️</button>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
            html += `</tbody></table>`;
            html += renderResultsFooter(startItem, endItem, totalItems, totalPages);
            list.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderResultsFooter(startItem, endItem, totalItems, totalPages) {
            const pageButtons = getPaginationItems(totalPages, currentPage).map(item => {
                if (item === 'ellipsis') {
                    return `<span class="pagination-ellipsis">…</span>`;
                }

                return `
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="pagination-page ${item === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}"
                        onclick="goToPage(${item})"
                        aria-label="Ir para página ${item}"
                        aria-current="${item === currentPage ? 'page' : 'false'}"
                    >
                        ${item}
                    </button>
                `;
            }).join('');

            return `
                <div class="results-footer">
                    <div class="page-size-control">
                        <label for="itemsPerPage">Exibir</label>
                        <select id="itemsPerPage" onchange="setItemsPerPage(this.value)">
                            ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.map(option => `
                                <option value="${option}" ${option === itemsPerPage ? 'selected' : ''}>${option}</option>
                            `).join('')}
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="pagination" aria-label="Paginação da lista de clientes">
                        <button type="button" class="pagination-btn" onclick="goToPage(1)" ${currentPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} aria-label="Primeira página">«</button>
                        <button type="button" class="pagination-btn" onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})" ${currentPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} aria-label="Página anterior">‹</button>
                        ${pageButtons}
                        <button type="button" class="pagination-btn" onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})" ${currentPage === totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} aria-label="Próxima página">›</button>
                        <button type="button" class="pagination-btn" onclick="goToPage(${totalPages})" ${currentPage === totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} aria-label="Última página">»</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="results-summary">${startItem} a ${endItem} de ${totalItems}</div>
                </div>
            `;
        }

        function getPaginationItems(totalPages, activePage) {
            if (totalPages <= 7) {
                return Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, index) => index + 1);
            }

            const pages = new Set([1, totalPages, activePage - 1, activePage, activePage + 1]);
            if (activePage <= 3) {
                pages.add(2);
                pages.add(3);
            }
            if (activePage >= totalPages - 2) {
                pages.add(totalPages - 1);
                pages.add(totalPages - 2);
            }

            const sortedPages = Array.from(pages)
                .filter(page => page >= 1 && page <= totalPages)
                .sort((a, b) => a - b);

            const result = [];
            sortedPages.forEach((page, index) => {
                if (index > 0 && page - sortedPages[index - 1] > 1) {
                    result.push('ellipsis');
                }
                result.push(page);
            });

            return result;
        }

        function goToPage(page) {
            const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredClients.length / itemsPerPage));
            currentPage = Math.min(Math.max(page, 1), totalPages);
            renderTable(filteredClients);
        }

        function setItemsPerPage(value) {
            itemsPerPage = Number(value) || 20;
            currentPage = 1;
            renderTable(filteredClients);
        }

        // --- FUNÇÕES DE MODAL (CRUD) ---
        async function openClientModal(client = null) {
            const modal = document.getElementById('clientModal');
            const title = document.getElementById('modalTitle');
            const container = document.getElementById('customFieldsContainer');
            container.innerHTML = '';
            document.getElementById('clientProductsContainer').innerHTML = '';
            const selectedPlan = client && client.server_name ? client.server_name : '';
            await Promise.all([loadPlanOptions(selectedPlan), loadProductCatalog()]);

            if (client) {
                title.textContent = "Editar Cliente";
                document.getElementById('clientId').value = client.id;
                document.getElementById('clientName').value = client.name;
                document.getElementById('clientLogin').value = client.login;
                document.getElementById('clientWhatsapp').value = client.whatsapp;
                document.getElementById('clientNotificationChannel').value = client.notification_channel || 'whatsapp';
                document.getElementById('clientExpiration').value = client.expiration_date;

                // --- NOVA LINHA: Carrega os dias configurados ou usa 3 como padrão ---
                document.getElementById('clientReminderDays').value = client.reminder_days_before || 3;
                // --------------------------------------------------------------------
                document.getElementById('clientServer').value = client.server_name || '';
                document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice').value = client.plan_price != null
                    ? formatCurrencyInput(client.plan_price)
                    : '';
                if (!document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice').value) {
                    syncPlanPriceWithSelection(true);
                }
                for (const product of (Array.isArray(client.selected_products) ? client.selected_products : [])) {
                    await addProductSelectionRow(product);
                }
                document.getElementById('clientNotes').value = client.notes || '';
                if (client.custom_fields) {
                    Object.entries(client.custom_fields).forEach(([key, value]) => addCustomFieldInput(key, value));
                }
            } else {
                title.textContent = "Novo Cliente";
                document.getElementById('clientId').value = '';
                document.querySelectorAll('#clientModal input, #clientModal textarea').forEach(i => i.value = '');
                document.getElementById('clientNotificationChannel').value = 'whatsapp';
                document.getElementById('clientServer').value = '';
                document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice').value = '';

                // --- NOVA LINHA: Define o padrão como 3 para novos clientes ---
                document.getElementById('clientReminderDays').value = '3';
                // --------------------------------------------------------------
            }
            updateProductsHint();
            updateClientTotalAmount();
            modal.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function openClientModalFromEncoded(encoded) {
            return openClientModal(decodeClientData(encoded));
        }

        function closeClientModal() { document.getElementById('clientModal').style.display = 'none'; }

        function addCustomFieldInput(key = '', value = '') {
            const container = document.getElementById('customFieldsContainer');
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'dynamic-field-row';
            div.innerHTML = `
                <input type="text" placeholder="Nome do Campo" class="field-key" value="${key}">
                <input type="text" placeholder="Valor" class="field-value" value="${value}">
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">X</button>
            `;
            container.appendChild(div);
        }

        async function saveClient() {
            const id = document.getElementById('clientId').value;
            const customFields = {};
            document.querySelectorAll('.dynamic-field-row').forEach(row => {
                const key = row.querySelector('.field-key').value.trim();
                const val = row.querySelector('.field-value').value.trim();
                if (key) customFields[key] = val;
            });

            const data = {
                name: document.getElementById('clientName').value,
                login: document.getElementById('clientLogin').value,
                whatsapp: document.getElementById('clientWhatsapp').value,
                notification_channel: document.getElementById('clientNotificationChannel').value,
                expiration_date: document.getElementById('clientExpiration').value,

                // --- NOVA LINHA: Envia a configuração de dias para o backend ---
                reminder_days_before: document.getElementById('clientReminderDays').value,
                // ---------------------------------------------------------------

                server_name: document.getElementById('clientServer').value.trim(),
                plan_price: parseCurrencyValue(document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice').value),
                selected_products: getSelectedProductsPayload(),
                notes: document.getElementById('clientNotes').value,
                custom_fields: customFields
            };

            if (!data.name || !data.login || !data.expiration_date) { alert("Preencha os campos obrigatórios."); return; }
            if (document.getElementById('clientPlanPrice').value.trim() && data.plan_price === null) { alert("Informe um valor válido no campo R$."); return; }
            const selectedProductsError = validateSelectedProductsRows();
            if (selectedProductsError) { alert(selectedProductsError); return; }

            const method = id ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
            const url = id ? `/clients/${id}` : '/clients/';

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(url, { method: method, body: JSON.stringify(data) });
                if (response.ok) {
                    closeClientModal();
                    loadClients();
                    showNotification(id ? "Cliente atualizado!" : "Cliente criado!", "success");
                } else { alert("Erro ao salvar."); }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); alert("Erro de conexão."); }
        }

        async function deleteClient(id) {
            if(!confirm("Tem certeza que deseja excluir este cliente?")) return;
            try {
                await apiFetch(`/clients/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                loadClients();
                showNotification("Cliente removido.", "info");
            } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        function openDetailsModal(client) {
            const modal = document.getElementById('detailsModal');
            const content = document.getElementById('detailsContent');
            let customFieldsHtml = '';
            if (client.custom_fields && Object.keys(client.custom_fields).length > 0) {
                customFieldsHtml = `<h4 style="margin-top:10px; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">Infos Adicionais</h4>`;
                Object.entries(client.custom_fields).forEach(([key, value]) => {
                    customFieldsHtml += `<div><strong>${key}:</strong> ${value}</div>`;
                });
            }
            const channelIcon = client.notification_channel === 'telegram' ? '✈️ Telegram' : '📱 WhatsApp';
            const errorHtml = hasReminderError(client)
                ? `
                    <div class="error-detail-card">
                        <strong>❗ Último erro de envio</strong>
                        <div>${escapeHtml(client.reminder_error_message)}</div>
                    </div>
                `
                : '';
            const selectedProducts = Array.isArray(client.selected_products) ? client.selected_products : [];
            const productsHtml = selectedProducts.length
                ? `
                    <div style="margin-top:10px;">
                        <h4 style="margin-bottom:0.65rem; border-bottom:1px solid #eee; padding-bottom:0.35rem;">Produtos adicionais</h4>
                        <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:0.5rem;">
                            ${selectedProducts.map((product) => `
                                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; gap:1rem; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:8px; padding:0.65rem 0.8rem;">
                                    <span>${escapeHtml(product.name || '-')}</span>
                                    <strong>${product.price != null ? formatMoneyDisplay(product.price) : '-'}</strong>
                                </div>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `
                : '';
            const totalAmount = getClientTotalAmount(client);

            content.innerHTML = `
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 10px;">
                    <div><strong>Nome:</strong> ${client.name}</div>
                    <div><strong>Login:</strong> ${client.login}</div>
                    <div><strong>WhatsApp:</strong> ${client.whatsapp}</div>
                    <div><strong>Canal:</strong> ${channelIcon}</div>
                    <div><strong>Vencimento:</strong> ${formatLocalDate(client.expiration_date)}</div>
                    <div><strong>Plano:</strong> ${client.server_name}</div>
                    <div><strong>Valor do plano:</strong> ${client.plan_price != null ? formatMoneyDisplay(client.plan_price) : '-'}</div>
                </div>
                ${productsHtml}
                <div style="margin-top:10px; padding:0.9rem 1rem; border-radius:12px; background:rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12); border:1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);">
                    <div style="font-size:0.8rem; font-weight:700; color:#047857; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:0.04em;">Valor somado de todos</div>
                    <div style="font-size:1.35rem; font-weight:800; color:#065f46; margin-top:0.2rem;">${formatMoneyDisplay(totalAmount)}</div>
                </div>
                ${client.notes ? `<div style="margin-top:10px; background:#fffbe6; padding:10px; border:1px solid #ffe58f; border-radius:5px;"><strong>Obs:</strong> ${client.notes}</div>` : ''}
                ${errorHtml}
                ${customFieldsHtml}
            `;
            modal.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function openDetailsModalFromEncoded(encoded) {
            openDetailsModal(decodeClientData(encoded));
        }
        function closeDetailsModal() { document.getElementById('detailsModal').style.display = 'none'; }

        // ==========================================
        // LÓGICA DE AGENDAMENTO E ENVIO MANUAL
        // ==========================================

        async function loadScheduleStatus() {
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/users/me/schedule');
                if (response.ok) {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    // Atualiza o texto do horário
                    document.getElementById('displayScheduleTime').textContent = data.time || "--:--";
                    document.getElementById('scheduleTime').value = data.time || "09:00";
                    document.getElementById('displayLastRun').textContent = formatDateTime(data.last_run_at);

                    // Atualiza o Toggle (Switch)
                    document.getElementById('scheduleToggle').checked = data.enabled;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Erro ao carregar agendamento:", error);
            }
        }

        async function toggleSchedule() {
            const isEnabled = document.getElementById('scheduleToggle').checked;
            const time = document.getElementById('displayScheduleTime').textContent;

            try {
                // Envia a atualização para o backend
                await apiFetch('/users/me/schedule', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ time: time, enabled: isEnabled })
                });
                showNotification(isEnabled ? "Agendamento ativado!" : "Agendamento desativado.", isEnabled ? "success" : "warning");
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                alert("Erro ao atualizar status.");
                // Reverte o switch em caso de erro
                document.getElementById('scheduleToggle').checked = !isEnabled;
            }
        }

        function openScheduleModal() { document.getElementById('scheduleModal').style.display = 'flex'; }
        function closeScheduleModal() { document.getElementById('scheduleModal').style.display = 'none'; }

        async function saveSchedule() {
            const time = document.getElementById('scheduleTime').value;
            const isEnabled = document.getElementById('scheduleToggle').checked;

            if (!time) { alert("Selecione um horário."); return; }

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/users/me/schedule', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ time: time, enabled: isEnabled })
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    document.getElementById('displayScheduleTime').textContent = time;
                    showNotification(`Horário atualizado para ${time}`, "success");
                    closeScheduleModal();
                } else { alert("Erro ao salvar."); }
            } catch (error) { console.error(error); alert("Erro de conexão."); }
        }

        // --- ENVIO MANUAL ---
        async function sendManualReminder(clientId) {
            if(!confirm("Deseja enviar a mensagem de cobrança agora? \n(A mensagem seguirá a mesma regra do agendador baseada na data de vencimento)")) return;

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(`/clients/${clientId}/remind`, { method: 'POST' });
                const result = await response.json();
                await loadClients();

                if (response.ok) {
                    showNotification(result.message, "success");
                } else {
                    // Mostra aviso se não houver critério (ex: faltam 10 dias)
                    if (isNotificationIntegrationMessage(result.detail)) {
                        showTopAlertBanner(result.detail);
                    } else {
                        alert(result.detail || "Erro ao enviar.");
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                alert("Erro ao processar envio.");
            }
        }

        // --- ENVIO EM MASSA (EXECUÇÃO MANUAL DA ROTINA) ---

        // 1. Abre o modal para escolher o filtro
        async function runBatchProcess() {
            await updateBatchFilterWithMessages();
            updateBatchRangeHint();
            document.getElementById('batchModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        // 2. Fecha o modal
        function closeBatchModal() {
            document.getElementById('batchModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        // 3. Executa o envio com o filtro selecionado
        async function executeBatchProcess() {
            const filterType = document.getElementById('batchFilter').value;
            const query = new URLSearchParams({ filter_type: filterType });

            if (expirationDaysAhead !== '') {
                query.set('days_ahead', expirationDaysAhead);
            } else if (expirationRangeStart) {
                query.set('date_from', expirationRangeStart);
            }

            if (expirationDaysAhead === '' && expirationRangeEnd) {
                query.set('date_to', expirationRangeEnd);
            }

            closeBatchModal();
            showNotification("Iniciando envio em massa...", "info");

            try {
                // Enviamos o filtro via Query Parameter para o backend
                // Ex: /clients/process-now?filter_type=3
                const response = await apiFetch(`/clients/process-now?${query.toString()}`, { method: 'POST' });
                const result = await response.json();
                await loadClients();
                await loadScheduleStatus();

                if (response.ok) {
                    showNotification(result.message, "success");
                } else {
                    if (isNotificationIntegrationMessage(result.detail)) {
                        showTopAlertBanner(result.detail);
                    } else {
                        alert(result.detail || "Erro ao processar.");
                    }
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                alert("Erro de conexão ao tentar executar a rotina.");
            }
        }

        function updateBatchRangeHint() {
            const hint = document.getElementById('batchRangeHint');
            if (!hint) return;

            if (expirationDaysAhead !== '') {
                const daysWindow = getExpirationDaysWindow(expirationDaysAhead);
                if (!daysWindow) {
                    hint.style.display = 'none';
                    hint.textContent = '';
                    return;
                }

                if (daysWindow.inputDays <= 1) {
                    hint.textContent = 'O envio em massa ficar\u00e1 restrito aos clientes que vencem hoje.';
                } else {
                    hint.textContent = `O envio em massa ficar\u00e1 restrito aos clientes vencidos h\u00e1 at\u00e9 ${daysWindow.maxDiff} dia${daysWindow.maxDiff === 1 ? '' : 's'} e aos que vencem nos pr\u00f3ximos ${daysWindow.maxDiff} dia${daysWindow.maxDiff === 1 ? '' : 's'}.`;
                }
                hint.style.display = 'block';
                return;
            }

            if (!expirationRangeStart && !expirationRangeEnd) {
                hint.style.display = 'none';
                hint.textContent = '';
                return;
            }

            const startLabel = expirationRangeStart
                ? formatLocalDate(expirationRangeStart)
                : 'o início';
            const endLabel = expirationRangeEnd
                ? formatLocalDate(expirationRangeEnd)
                : 'o fim';

            hint.textContent = `O envio em massa ficará restrito aos clientes com vencimento entre ${startLabel} e ${endLabel}.`;
            hint.style.display = 'block';
        }

        // Adiciona opções de mensagens personalizadas ao select de Critério de Envio
        async function updateBatchFilterWithMessages() {
            const batchFilter = document.getElementById('batchFilter');
            if (!batchFilter) return;
            const currentValue = batchFilter.value;
            // Salva as opções padrão
            const defaultOptions = [
                { value: 'default', text: '⚡ Padrão (Respeitar configuração de cada cliente)' },
                { value: '3', text: '📅 Vencimento em 3 Dias' },
                { value: '2', text: '📅 Vencimento em 2 Dias' },
                { value: '1', text: '⏰ Vencimento em 1 Dia (Amanhã)' },
                { value: '0', text: '🚨 Vencimento Hoje' },
                { value: 'expired', text: '❌ Vencidos (Ontem ou antes)' }
            ];
            // Limpa o select
            batchFilter.innerHTML = '';
            // Adiciona as opções padrão
            defaultOptions.forEach(opt => {
                const option = document.createElement('option');
                option.value = opt.value;
                option.textContent = opt.text;
                batchFilter.appendChild(option);
            });

            // Adiciona mensagens personalizadas configuradas em /messages.html
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch('/users/me');
                if (response.ok) {
                    const user = await response.json();
                    const customMessages = Array.isArray(user.reminder_scenarios)
                        ? user.reminder_scenarios.filter(msg => msg && typeof msg === 'object' && String(msg.template || '').trim())
                        : [];

                    customMessages.forEach(msg => {
                        const option = document.createElement('option');
                        option.value = `custom:${msg.id}`;
                        option.textContent = `💬 Mensagem personalizada: ${msg.name || 'Sem nome'}`;
                        batchFilter.appendChild(option);
                    });
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Falha ao carregar mensagens personalizadas para envio em massa.', e);
            }

            const availableValues = Array.from(batchFilter.options).map(option => option.value);
            batchFilter.value = availableValues.includes(currentValue) ? currentValue : 'default';
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                            <ol style="margin-left: 1.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                                <li>Abra o Telegram e pesquise por <strong>@BotFather</strong>.</li>
                                <li>Envie o comando <code>/newbot</code>.</li>
                                <li>Escolha um nome para o bot (ex: <em>Meu Monitor</em>).</li>
                                <li>Escolha um username (deve terminar em 'bot', ex: <em>meu_monitor_bot</em>).</li>
                                <li>O BotFather enviará o <strong>Token</strong>. Copie e cole no campo acima.</li>
                            </ol>
                        </div>
                        <!-- Passo 2 -->
                        <div style="background: #f8fafc; padding: 1rem; border-radius: 8px; border-left: 4px solid var(--success);">
                            <h4 style="margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">2. Descobrir o Chat ID</h4>
                            <p style="font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Dependendo de onde você quer receber os alertas:</p>
                            <details>
                                <summary style="cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);">👤 Para você (Mensagem Privada)</summary>
                                <p style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                                    1. Inicie uma conversa com seu novo bot (clique em Começar/Start).<br>
                                    2. Pesquise por <strong>@userinfobot</strong> no Telegram.<br>
                                    3. Envie qualquer mensagem para ele.<br>
                                    4. Ele responderá com seu ID (ex: <code>123456789</code>). Copie este número.
                                </p>
                            </details>
                            <details style="margin-top: 0.5rem;">
                                <summary style="cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);">👥 Para um Grupo</summary>
                                <p style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                                    1. Adicione o seu bot no grupo desejado.<br>
                                    2. Adicione também o bot <strong>@RawDataBot</strong> no grupo.<br>
                                    3. Assim que entrar, o RawDataBot enviará um JSON. Procure por <code>"chat": { "id": -100... }</code>.<br>
                                    4. Copie esse ID (incluindo o sinal de menos).<br>
                                    5. Remova o RawDataBot do grupo depois.
                                </p>
                            </details>
                            <details style="margin-top: 0.5rem;">
                                <summary style="cursor: pointer; font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary);">📢 Para um Canal</summary>
                                <p style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                                    1. Adicione o bot como <strong>Administrador</strong> do canal.<br>
                                    2. Encaminhe uma mensagem qualquer desse canal para o bot <strong>@getidsbot</strong>.<br>
                                    3. Ele retornará o ID do canal (começa com -100).
                                </p>
                            </details>
                        </div>
                    </div>
