    <script>
        const HEADER_ALIASES = {
            name: ['name', 'nome', 'cliente', 'client'],
            login: ['login', 'usuario', 'usuário', 'username', 'user'],
            server_name: ['server_name', 'servidor', 'server', 'painel', 'servidor_opcional'],
            whatsapp: ['whatsapp', 'telefone', 'celular', 'phone', 'numero', 'número'],
            expiration_date: ['expiration_date', 'vencimento', 'validade', 'data_vencimento', 'expiracao', 'expiração'],
            notes: ['notes', 'observacoes', 'observações', 'obs', 'anotacoes', 'anotações'],
            m3u8_url: ['m3u8_url', 'm3u8', 'playlist', 'url_m3u8'],
            notification_channel: ['notification_channel', 'canal', 'canal_notificacao', 'canal_notificação'],
            reminder_days_before: ['reminder_days_before', 'dias_aviso', 'dias_antes', 'aviso', 'lembrete'],
            notify_downtime: ['notify_downtime', 'alerta_queda', 'queda'],
            reminder_enabled: ['reminder_enabled', 'cobranca_automatica', 'cobrança_automática', 'cobranca'],
            notify_after_expiration: ['notify_after_expiration', 'avisar_vencidos', 'avisar_apos_vencer', 'avisar_apos_vencer']
        };

        let previewRows = [];
        let existingClients = [];
        let importInProgress = false;
        let droppedFile = null;

        function normalizeHeader(value) {
            return String(value || '')
                .normalize('NFD')
                .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
                .toLowerCase()
                .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_')
                .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '');
        }

        function normalizeText(value) {
            return String(value || '').trim();
        }

        function normalizePhone(value) {
            return String(value || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
        }

        function parseBoolean(value, fallback) {
            if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') return fallback;
            const normalized = normalizeHeader(value);
            if (['1', 'true', 'sim', 'yes', 'ativo', 'enabled'].includes(normalized)) return true;
            if (['0', 'false', 'nao', 'não', 'no', 'inativo', 'disabled'].includes(normalized)) return false;
            return fallback;
        }

        function parseDate(value) {
            const raw = normalizeText(value);
            if (!raw) return '';

            if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw)) return raw;

            const dateParts = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{2,4})$/);
            if (dateParts) {
                const day = dateParts[1].padStart(2, '0');
                const month = dateParts[2].padStart(2, '0');
                const year = dateParts[3].length === 2 ? `20${dateParts[3]}` : dateParts[3];
                return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
            }

            if (/^\d{5}$/.test(raw)) {
                const excelEpoch = new Date(Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30));
                excelEpoch.setUTCDate(excelEpoch.getUTCDate() + Number(raw));
                return excelEpoch.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
            }

            const parsed = new Date(raw);
            if (!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
                return parsed.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
            }

            return '';
        }

        function formatDisplayDate(value) {
            if (!value) return '-';
            const parts = value.split('-');
            if (parts.length !== 3) return value;
            return `${parts[2]}/${parts[1]}/${parts[0]}`;
        }

        function detectSeparator(text) {
            const firstLine = String(text || '').split(/\r?\n/).find((line) => line.trim());
            if (!firstLine) return ';';
            const candidates = [';', ',', '\t'];
            return candidates
                .map((separator) => ({ separator, count: firstLine.split(separator).length }))
                .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)[0].separator;
        }

        function splitCsvLine(line, separator) {
            const values = [];
            let current = '';
            let inQuotes = false;

            for (let index = 0; index < line.length; index += 1) {
                const char = line[index];
                const next = line[index + 1];

                if (char === '"') {
                    if (inQuotes && next === '"') {
                        current += '"';
                        index += 1;
                    } else {
                        inQuotes = !inQuotes;
                    }
                } else if (char === separator && !inQuotes) {
                    values.push(current.trim());
                    current = '';
                } else {
                    current += char;
                }
            }

            values.push(current.trim());
            return values;
        }

        function parseCsv(text) {
            const separator = detectSeparator(text);
            const lines = String(text || '')
                .split(/\r?\n/)
                .map((line) => line.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);

            if (lines.length < 2) {
                return [];
            }

            const rawHeaders = splitCsvLine(lines[0], separator);
            const normalizedHeaders = rawHeaders.map(normalizeHeader);

            return lines.slice(1).map((line, index) => {
                const columns = splitCsvLine(line, separator);
                const row = { __line: index + 2 };
                normalizedHeaders.forEach((header, headerIndex) => {
                    row[header || `coluna_${headerIndex + 1}`] = columns[headerIndex] || '';
                });
                return row;
            });
        }

        function findMappedKey(headers, canonicalKey) {
            const aliases = HEADER_ALIASES[canonicalKey] || [];
            return headers.find((header) => aliases.includes(header)) || '';
        }

        function buildImportPayload(rawRow, duplicateKeysInBatch) {
            const headers = Object.keys(rawRow).filter((key) => !key.startsWith('__'));
            const mappedKeys = {};

            Object.keys(HEADER_ALIASES).forEach((canonicalKey) => {
                mappedKeys[canonicalKey] = findMappedKey(headers, canonicalKey);
            });

            const payload = {
                name: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.name]),
                login: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.login]),
                server_name: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.server_name]) || null,
                whatsapp: normalizePhone(rawRow[mappedKeys.whatsapp]),
                expiration_date: parseDate(rawRow[mappedKeys.expiration_date]),
                notes: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.notes]),
                m3u8_url: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.m3u8_url]),
                notification_channel: normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.notification_channel]) || document.getElementById('defaultChannel').value,
                reminder_days_before: String(normalizeText(rawRow[mappedKeys.reminder_days_before]) || document.getElementById('defaultReminderDays').value || '3'),
                notify_downtime: parseBoolean(rawRow[mappedKeys.notify_downtime], document.getElementById('defaultNotifyDowntime').checked),
                reminder_enabled: parseBoolean(rawRow[mappedKeys.reminder_enabled], document.getElementById('defaultReminderEnabled').checked),
                notify_after_expiration: parseBoolean(rawRow[mappedKeys.notify_after_expiration], document.getElementById('defaultNotifyAfterExpiration').checked)
            };

            const customFields = {};
            headers.forEach((header) => {
                const isMapped = Object.values(mappedKeys).includes(header);
                const value = normalizeText(rawRow[header]);
                if (!isMapped && value) {
                    customFields[header] = value;
                }
            });
            if (Object.keys(customFields).length) {
                payload.custom_fields = customFields;
            }

            const errors = [];
            if (!payload.name) errors.push('Nome ausente');
            if (!payload.login) errors.push('Login ausente');
            if (!payload.whatsapp) errors.push('WhatsApp ausente');
            if (!payload.expiration_date) errors.push('Vencimento inválido');

            const existingDuplicate = existingClients.find((client) =>
                normalizeText(client.login).toLowerCase() === payload.login.toLowerCase() ||
                normalizePhone(client.whatsapp) === payload.whatsapp
            );

            const batchKey = `${payload.login.toLowerCase()}::${payload.whatsapp}`;
            const duplicateInBatch = duplicateKeysInBatch.has(batchKey);

            if (!errors.length && !duplicateInBatch) {
                duplicateKeysInBatch.add(batchKey);
            }

            let status = 'ready';
            let statusLabel = 'Pronto';

            if (errors.length) {
                status = 'error';
                statusLabel = errors.join(' • ');
            } else if (existingDuplicate) {
                status = 'duplicate';
                statusLabel = `Já existe: ${existingDuplicate.name || existingDuplicate.login}`;
            } else if (duplicateInBatch) {
                status = 'duplicate';
                statusLabel = 'Duplicado na própria planilha';
            }

            return {
                line: rawRow.__line,
                payload,
                status,
                statusLabel
            };
        }

        function updateSummary(rows) {
            const total = rows.length;
            const ready = rows.filter((row) => row.status === 'ready').length;
            const duplicates = rows.filter((row) => row.status === 'duplicate').length;
            const errors = rows.filter((row) => row.status === 'error').length;

            document.getElementById('summaryTotal').textContent = total;
            document.getElementById('summaryReady').textContent = ready;
            document.getElementById('summaryDuplicates').textContent = duplicates;
            document.getElementById('summaryErrors').textContent = errors;
            document.getElementById('btnImport').disabled = ready === 0 || importInProgress;
        }

        function statusClass(status) {
            if (status === 'ready') return 'status-badge status-ok';
            if (status === 'duplicate') return 'status-badge status-warn';
            return 'status-badge status-error';
        }

        function renderPreview(rows) {
            const emptyState = document.getElementById('emptyState');
            const wrapper = document.getElementById('previewWrapper');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('previewTableBody');

            if (!rows.length) {
                emptyState.style.display = '';
                wrapper.style.display = 'none';
                tbody.innerHTML = '';
                updateSummary([]);
                return;
            }

            emptyState.style.display = 'none';
            wrapper.style.display = '';
            tbody.innerHTML = rows.map((row) => `
                <tr>
                    <td>${row.line}</td>
                    <td><span class="${statusClass(row.status)}">${escapeHtml(row.statusLabel)}</span></td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(row.payload.name || '-')}</td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(row.payload.login || '-')}</td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(row.payload.server_name || '-')}</td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(row.payload.whatsapp || '-')}</td>
                    <td>${escapeHtml(formatDisplayDate(row.payload.expiration_date))}</td>
                    <td class="notes-cell">${escapeHtml(row.payload.notes || '-')}</td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');

            updateSummary(rows);
        }

        async function loadExistingClients() {
            const response = await apiFetch('/clients/');
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar clientes já cadastrados.');
            existingClients = await response.json();
        }

        async function buildPreview() {
            const resultBox = document.getElementById('importResult');
            resultBox.style.display = 'none';
            resultBox.innerHTML = '';

            const pasted = document.getElementById('rawInput').value.trim();
            const fileInput = document.getElementById('csvFile');
            let sourceText = pasted;

            if (!sourceText && droppedFile) {
                sourceText = await droppedFile.text();
            }

            if (!sourceText && fileInput.files.length) {
                sourceText = await fileInput.files[0].text();
            }

            if (!sourceText) {
                notify('Cole os dados ou selecione um CSV antes de gerar a prévia.', 'warning');
                return;
            }

            await loadExistingClients();
            const parsedRows = parseCsv(sourceText);

            if (!parsedRows.length) {
                previewRows = [];
                renderPreview(previewRows);
                notify('Não foi possível identificar linhas válidas na planilha.', 'warning');
                return;
            }

            const batchKeys = new Set();
            previewRows = parsedRows.map((row) => buildImportPayload(row, batchKeys));
            renderPreview(previewRows);
        }

        async function importClients() {
            if (importInProgress) return;

            const rowsToImport = previewRows.filter((row) => row.status === 'ready');
            if (!rowsToImport.length) {
                notify('Não há clientes prontos para importar.', 'warning');
                return;
            }

            importInProgress = true;
            document.getElementById('btnImport').disabled = true;

            let created = 0;
            const failures = [];
            const importedLines = new Set();

            for (const row of rowsToImport) {
                try {
                    const response = await apiFetch('/clients/', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        body: JSON.stringify(row.payload)
                    });

                    if (!response.ok) {
                        let message = `Erro ${response.status}`;
                        try {
                            const errorData = await response.json();
                            message = errorData.detail || message;
                        } catch (error) {
                            // Mantém o fallback quando a API não retornar JSON.
                        }
                        failures.push(`Linha ${row.line}: ${message}`);
                    } else {
                        created += 1;
                        importedLines.add(row.line);
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    failures.push(`Linha ${row.line}: ${error.message || 'Falha ao importar'}`);
                }
            }

            importInProgress = false;
            previewRows = previewRows.map((row) => importedLines.has(row.line)
                ? Object.assign({}, row, { status: 'duplicate', statusLabel: 'Importado nesta execução' })
                : row);
            renderPreview(previewRows);
            await loadExistingClients();

            const resultBox = document.getElementById('importResult');
            resultBox.style.display = '';

            if (!failures.length) {
                resultBox.className = 'alert alert-success mt-3';
                resultBox.innerHTML = `<strong>Importação concluída.</strong> ${created} cliente(s) criado(s) com sucesso.`;
                notify(`Importação concluída: ${created} cliente(s) criado(s).`, 'success');
                return;
            }

            resultBox.className = 'alert alert-warning mt-3';
            resultBox.innerHTML = `
                <strong>Importação parcial.</strong> ${created} cliente(s) criado(s).
                <div class="mt-2">${failures.map((failure) => `<div>${escapeHtml(failure)}</div>`).join('')}</div>
            `;
            notify(`Importação parcial: ${created} cliente(s) criado(s) e ${failures.length} falha(s).`, 'warning');
        }

        function downloadTemplate() {
            const template = [
                'nome;login;servidor_opcional;whatsapp;vencimento;observacoes;m3u8;canal;dias_aviso',
                'Maria Silva;maria01;Servidor 1;5599999999999;30/07/2026;Cliente premium;https://exemplo.com/lista.m3u8;whatsapp;3'
            ].join('\n');

            const blob = new Blob([template], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.href = url;
            link.download = 'modelo-importacao-clientes.csv';
            document.body.appendChild(link);
            link.click();
            link.remove();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }

        function bindDragAndDrop() {
            const dropzone = document.getElementById('dropzone');
            const fileInput = document.getElementById('csvFile');

            ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach((eventName) => {
                dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    dropzone.classList.add('is-dragover');
                });
            });

            ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach((eventName) => {
                dropzone.addEventListener(eventName, (event) => {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    dropzone.classList.remove('is-dragover');
                });
            });

            dropzone.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
                const files = event.dataTransfer ? event.dataTransfer.files : null;
                if (files && files.length) {
                    droppedFile = files[0];
                }
            });

            fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
                droppedFile = null;
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            bindDragAndDrop();
            document.getElementById('btnTemplate').addEventListener('click', downloadTemplate);
            document.getElementById('btnPreview').addEventListener('click', () => {
                buildPreview().catch((error) => {
                    notify(error.message || 'Falha ao gerar prévia.', 'error');
                });
            });
            document.getElementById('btnImport').addEventListener('click', () => {
                importClients().catch((error) => {
                    importInProgress = false;
                    updateSummary(previewRows);
                    notify(error.message || 'Falha ao importar clientes.', 'error');
                });
            });
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="pt-BR">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Login | Gestor Nexus</title>
    <!-- Link para o CSS principal -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="assets/css/style.css">
</head>
<body class="auth-body">

    <div class="auth-container">
        <div class="auth-card">
            <div class="auth-header">
                <div class="brand-logo">GESTOR<span>NEXUS</span></div>
                <p>Entre para acessar o painel</p>
            </div>

            <form id="loginForm" onsubmit="handleLogin(event)">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="email">E-mail</label>
                    <input type="email" id="email" placeholder="seu@email.com" required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="password">Senha</label>
                    <input type="password" id="password" placeholder="Sua senha" required>
                </div>

                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-block">Entrar</button>
            </form>

            <div class="auth-footer">
                <p>Não tem uma conta? <a href="register.html">Criar conta</a></p>
            </div>

            <div id="message" style="margin-top: 15px; text-align: center; font-size: 0.9rem; min-height: 20px;"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function resolveApiBase() {
            if (window.location.protocol === 'file:') {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            if (['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname)) {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            return window.location.origin;
        }

        const API_URL = resolveApiBase();

        async function handleLogin(event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const msgDiv = document.getElementById('message');
            const submitButton = event.submitter || document.querySelector('#loginForm button[type="submit"]');
            let timeoutId;

            msgDiv.textContent = 'Autenticando...';
            msgDiv.style.color = '#64748b'; // Cor neutra
            if (submitButton) {
                submitButton.disabled = true;
                submitButton.textContent = 'Autenticando...';
            }

            try {
                // O padrão OAuth2 do FastAPI espera dados como form-urlencoded
                const formData = new URLSearchParams();
                formData.append('username', email); // O backend espera o campo 'username'
                formData.append('password', password);
                const controller = new AbortController();
                timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000);

                const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/token`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'
                    },
                    body: formData,
                    signal: controller.signal
                });
                clearTimeout(timeoutId);

                if (response.ok) {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    // Salva o token no LocalStorage
                    localStorage.setItem('token', data.access_token);

                    msgDiv.textContent = 'Login realizado! Redirecionando...';
                    msgDiv.style.color = 'var(--success)'; // Verde do tema

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = 'dashboard.html';
                    }, 1000);
                } else {
                    msgDiv.textContent = 'E-mail ou senha incorretos.';
                    msgDiv.style.color = 'var(--danger)'; // Vermelho do tema
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                msgDiv.textContent = error.name === 'AbortError'
                    ? 'A API demorou para responder. Reinicie o backend e tente novamente.'
                    : 'Erro de conexão com o servidor.';
                msgDiv.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
            } finally {
                if (timeoutId) {
                    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                }
                if (submitButton) {
                    submitButton.disabled = false;
                    submitButton.textContent = 'Entrar';
                }
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        function cycleLabel(value) {
            return {
                monthly: 'Mensal',
                quarterly: 'Trimestral',
                semiannual: 'Semestral',
                yearly: 'Anual'
            }[value] || value || '-';
        }

        function money(value) {
            const numeric = Number(value || 0);
            return numeric.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' });
        }

        function resetForm() {
            document.getElementById('planId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('planName').value = '';
            document.getElementById('planDescription').value = '';
            document.getElementById('planPrice').value = '';
            document.getElementById('planBillingCycle').value = 'monthly';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Novo plano';
        }

        function editPlan(plan) {
            document.getElementById('planId').value = plan.id;
            document.getElementById('planName').value = plan.name || '';
            document.getElementById('planDescription').value = plan.description || '';
            document.getElementById('planPrice').value = plan.price || '';
            document.getElementById('planBillingCycle').value = plan.billing_cycle || 'monthly';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Editar plano';
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        function editPlanFromEncoded(encoded) {
            editPlan(decodeItem(encoded));
        }

        async function loadPlans() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('plansTableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted">Carregando...</td></tr>';
            try {
                const [plansResp, productsResp] = await Promise.all([apiFetch('/plans'), apiFetch('/products')]);
                if (plansResp.status === 401 || productsResp.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                if (!plansResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar planos.');
                if (!productsResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar produtos.');
                const [items, products] = await Promise.all([plansResp.json(), productsResp.json()]);
                const usageMap = {};
                products.forEach((product) => {
                    if (product.plan_id) usageMap[product.plan_id] = (usageMap[product.plan_id] || 0) + 1;
                });
                document.getElementById('planCount').textContent = `${items.length} item(ns)`;
                if (!items.length) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted">Nenhum plano cadastrado.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }
                tbody.innerHTML = items.map((item) => `
                    <tr>
                        <td>
                            <div class="d-flex flex-column gap-1">
                                <strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong>
                                <span class="badge bg-light text-dark border align-self-start">${money(item.price)}</span>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>${usageMap[item.id] ? `<span class="badge bg-primary">${usageMap[item.id]} produto(s)</span>` : '<span class="text-muted">Livre</span>'}</td>
                        <td>${cycleLabel(item.billing_cycle)}</td>
                        <td>${item.description ? escapeHtml(item.description) : '<span class="text-muted">Sem descrição</span>'}</td>
                        <td>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick="editPlanFromEncoded('${encodeItem(item)}')">Editar</button>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="deletePlan(${item.id})">Excluir</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');
            } catch (error) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-danger">Erro ao carregar planos.</td></tr>';
            }
        }

        async function savePlan() {
            const id = document.getElementById('planId').value;
            const payload = {
                name: document.getElementById('planName').value.trim(),
                description: document.getElementById('planDescription').value.trim(),
                price: parseFloat(document.getElementById('planPrice').value),
                billing_cycle: document.getElementById('planBillingCycle').value
            };
            if (!payload.name) {
                notify('Informe o nome do plano.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!payload.price || payload.price <= 0) {
                notify('Informe um valor válido.', 'error');
                return;
            }

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(id ? `/plans/${id}` : '/plans', {
                    method: id ? 'PUT' : 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                if (!response.ok) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao salvar plano.');
                }
                notify(id ? 'Plano atualizado com sucesso.' : 'Plano criado com sucesso.', 'success');
                resetForm();
                loadPlans();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        async function deletePlan(id) {
            const row = document.querySelector(`button[onclick="deletePlan(${id})"]`);
            const usageCell = row ? row.closest('tr').children[1] : null;
            if (usageCell && usageCell.textContent.indexOf('produto') !== -1) {
                notify('Remova os produtos vinculados antes de excluir este plano.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!confirm('Deseja excluir este plano?')) return;
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(`/plans/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (!response.ok && response.status !== 204) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao excluir plano.');
                }
                notify('Plano excluído.', 'success');
                loadPlans();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadPlans);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-12 col-md-6">
                                    <div class="form-group h-100">
                                        <label class="form-label">Plano</label>
                                        <select id="productPlan" class="form-control"></select>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-12">
                                    <div class="rounded-3 border px-3 py-2 bg-light-subtle">
                                        <small class="text-muted">O valor é preenchido automaticamente quando um plano é selecionado.</small>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-12">
                                    <div class="form-group">
                                        <label class="form-label">Descrição</label>
                                        <textarea id="productDescription" class="form-control" rows="4" placeholder="Descrição do produto"></textarea>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="d-flex gap-2 justify-content-end mt-4 pt-3 border-top">
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="resetForm()">Limpar</button>

                        <div class="card">
                            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">

    <script>
        let planPriceMap = {};

        function syncPriceWithPlan() {
            const planId = document.getElementById('productPlan').value;
            const priceInput = document.getElementById('productPrice');
            if (!planId || !planPriceMap[planId]) {
                priceInput.value = '';
                return;
            }
            priceInput.value = Number(planPriceMap[planId]).toFixed(2);
        }

        document.addEventListener('change', function (event) {
            if (event.target && event.target.id === 'productPlan') {
                syncPriceWithPlan();
            }
        });

        function resetForm() {
            document.getElementById('productId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('productName').value = '';
            document.getElementById('productDescription').value = '';
            document.getElementById('productPrice').value = '';
            document.getElementById('productCategory').value = '';
            document.getElementById('productPlan').value = '';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Novo produto';
        }

        function editProduct(product) {
            document.getElementById('productId').value = product.id;
            document.getElementById('productName').value = product.name || '';
            document.getElementById('productDescription').value = product.description || '';
            document.getElementById('productPrice').value = product.price || '';
            document.getElementById('productCategory').value = product.category_id || '';
            document.getElementById('productPlan').value = product.plan_id || '';
            document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Editar produto';
            syncPriceWithPlan();
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        function editProductFromEncoded(encoded) {
            editProduct(decodeItem(encoded));
        }

        async function loadCategoriesIntoSelect(selectedId) {
            const select = document.getElementById('productCategory');
            const response = await apiFetch('/categories');
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar categorias.');
            const items = await response.json();
            select.innerHTML = items.length
                ? '<option value="">Selecione uma categoria</option>' + items.map((item) => `<option value="${item.id}">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</option>`).join('')
                : '<option value="">Nenhuma categoria cadastrada</option>';
            if (selectedId) select.value = selectedId;
        }

        async function loadPlansIntoSelect(selectedId) {
            const select = document.getElementById('productPlan');
            const response = await apiFetch('/plans');
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar planos.');
            const items = await response.json();
            planPriceMap = {};
            select.innerHTML = items.length
                ? '<option value="">Selecione um plano</option>' + items.map((item) => {
                    planPriceMap[item.id] = item.price;
                    return `<option value="${item.id}">${escapeHtml(item.name)} - ${money(item.price)}</option>`;
                }).join('')
                : '<option value="">Nenhum plano cadastrado</option>';
            if (selectedId) select.value = selectedId;
            syncPriceWithPlan();
        }

        async function loadProducts() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('productsTableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted">Carregando...</td></tr>';
            try {
                const [productsResp, categoriesResp, plansResp] = await Promise.all([apiFetch('/products'), apiFetch('/categories'), apiFetch('/plans')]);
                if (productsResp.status === 401 || categoriesResp.status === 401 || plansResp.status === 401) {
                    window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    return;
                }
                if (!productsResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar produtos.');
                if (!categoriesResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar categorias.');
                if (!plansResp.ok) throw new Error('Falha ao carregar planos.');
                const [products, categories, plans] = await Promise.all([productsResp.json(), categoriesResp.json(), plansResp.json()]);
                const categoryMap = {};
                categories.forEach((category) => { categoryMap[category.id] = category.name; });
                const planMap = {};
                plans.forEach((plan) => { planMap[plan.id] = plan.name; });
                document.getElementById('productCount').textContent = `${products.length} item(ns)`;
                await loadCategoriesIntoSelect(document.getElementById('productCategory').value);
                await loadPlansIntoSelect(document.getElementById('productPlan').value);
                if (!products.length) {
                    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted">Nenhum produto cadastrado.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }
                tbody.innerHTML = products.map((item) => `
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></td>
                        <td>
                            <div class="d-flex flex-column gap-1">
                                <span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">${escapeHtml(categoryMap[item.category_id] || 'Sem categoria')}</span>
                                <span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">${escapeHtml(planMap[item.plan_id] || 'Sem plano')}</span>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td>${money(item.price)}</td>
                        <td>${item.description ? escapeHtml(item.description) : '<span class="text-muted">Sem descrição</span>'}</td>
                        <td>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" onclick="editProductFromEncoded('${encodeItem(item)}')">Editar</button>
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" onclick="deleteProduct(${item.id})">Excluir</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');
            } catch (error) {
                tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-danger">Erro ao carregar produtos.</td></tr>';
            }
        }

        async function saveProduct() {
            const id = document.getElementById('productId').value;
            const payload = {
                name: document.getElementById('productName').value.trim(),
                description: document.getElementById('productDescription').value.trim(),
                price: parseFloat(document.getElementById('productPrice').value),
                category_id: parseInt(document.getElementById('productCategory').value, 10),
                plan_id: parseInt(document.getElementById('productPlan').value, 10)
            };
            if (!payload.name) {
                notify('Informe o nome do produto.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!payload.price || payload.price <= 0) {
                notify('Informe um valor válido.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!payload.category_id) {
                notify('Selecione uma categoria.', 'error');
                return;
            }
            if (!payload.plan_id) {
                notify('Selecione um plano.', 'error');
                return;
            }

            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(id ? `/products/${id}` : '/products', {
                    method: id ? 'PUT' : 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
                });
                if (!response.ok) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao salvar produto.');
                }
                notify(id ? 'Produto atualizado com sucesso.' : 'Produto criado com sucesso.', 'success');
                resetForm();
                loadProducts();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        async function deleteProduct(id) {
            if (!confirm('Deseja excluir este produto?')) return;
            try {
                const response = await apiFetch(`/products/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (!response.ok && response.status !== 204) {
                    const err = await response.json();
                    throw new Error(err.detail || 'Falha ao excluir produto.');
                }
                notify('Produto excluído.', 'success');
                loadProducts();
            } catch (error) {
                notify(error.message, 'error');
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadProducts);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="container">

    <script>
        function resolveApiBase() {
            if (window.location.protocol === 'file:') {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            if (['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname)) {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            return window.location.origin;
        }

        const API_URL = resolveApiBase();
        let checkInterval;

        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // Aguarda dados do QR Code serem passados pela janela pai
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (window.qrcodeData) {
                    displayQRCode(window.qrcodeData);
                    startStatusCheck();
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('qrContent').innerHTML =
                        '<p class="error">Erro ao carregar QR Code</p>';
                }
            }, 500);
        });

        function displayQRCode(qrcodeData) {
            const qrContent = document.getElementById('qrContent');

            // Verifica se tem base64
            if (qrcodeData.base64) {
                qrContent.innerHTML = `<img src="${qrcodeData.base64}" alt="QR Code">`;
            }
            // Ou se tem código texto
            else if (qrcodeData.code) {
                // Gera QR Code usando API pública
                const qrUrl = `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=300x300&data=${encodeURIComponent(qrcodeData.code)}`;
                qrContent.innerHTML = `<img src="${qrUrl}" alt="QR Code">`;
            }
            else {
                qrContent.innerHTML = '<p class="error">QR Code não disponível</p>';
            }
        }

        async function startStatusCheck() {
            // Verifica status a cada 3 segundos
            checkInterval = setInterval(checkConnectionStatus, 3000);

            // Timeout de 2 minutos
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (checkInterval) {
                    clearInterval(checkInterval);
                    document.getElementById('statusText').textContent =
                        'Tempo esgotado. Feche esta janela e tente novamente.';
                }
            }, 120000);
        }

        async function checkConnectionStatus() {
            try {
                const token = localStorage.getItem('token') || window.opener.localStorage.getItem('token');

                const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/whatsapp/status`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`
                    }
                });

                if (!response.ok) {
                    console.error('Erro ao verificar status');
                    return;
                }

                const data = await response.json();
                console.log('🔄 Status:', data);

                if (data.connected) {
                    clearInterval(checkInterval);

                    document.getElementById('qrContent').innerHTML =
                        '<p class="success">✅ Conectado com sucesso!</p>';
                    document.getElementById('statusText').textContent =
                        'Fechando em 2 segundos...';

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        // Atualiza dashboard da janela pai
                        if (window.opener && typeof window.opener.loadUserData === 'function') {
                            window.opener.loadUserData();
                        }
                        window.close();
                    }, 2000);
                }

            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao verificar status:', error);
            }
        }

        // Limpar interval ao fechar
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
            if (checkInterval) {
                clearInterval(checkInterval);
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>

                <p>Crie sua conta para começar a monitorar</p>
            </div>

            <form id="registerForm" onsubmit="handleRegister(event)">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="name">Nome Completo</label>
                    <input type="text" id="name" placeholder="Seu nome" required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="email">E-mail</label>
                    <input type="email" id="email" placeholder="seu@email.com" required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="password">Senha</label>
                    <input type="password" id="password" placeholder="Sua senha segura" required>
                </div>

                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-block">Criar Conta</button>
            </form>

            <div class="auth-footer">
                <p>Já tem uma conta? <a href="login.html">Fazer Login</a></p>
            </div>

            <div id="message" style="margin-top: 15px; text-align: center; font-size: 0.9rem;"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        function resolveApiBase() {
            if (window.location.protocol === 'file:') {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            if (['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname)) {
                return 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
            }

            return window.location.origin;
        }

        const API_URL = resolveApiBase();

        async function handleRegister(event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            const name = document.getElementById('name').value;
            const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const msgDiv = document.getElementById('message');

            msgDiv.textContent = 'Criando conta...';
            msgDiv.style.color = '#666';

            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/register`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ name, email, password })
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    msgDiv.textContent = 'Conta criada com sucesso! Redirecionando...';
                    msgDiv.style.color = 'green';
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = 'login.html';
                    }, 2000);
                } else {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    msgDiv.textContent = data.detail || 'Erro ao criar conta.';
                    msgDiv.style.color = 'red';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
                msgDiv.textContent = 'Erro de conexão com o servidor.';
                msgDiv.style.color = 'red';
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        const token = localStorage.getItem('token');

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
            if (!token) {
                window.location.href = 'login.html';
                return;
            }

            const currentUser = await loadSidebarInfo();
            if (!currentUser) return;

            if (['reseller', 'super_admin'].includes(currentUser.role)) {
                document.getElementById('reseller-panel').style.display = 'block';
                await loadResellers();
                await loadUsersToPromote();
            } else {
                document.getElementById('access-denied').style.display = 'block';
            }
        });

        async function loadResellers() {
            try {
                const resellers = await fetchAPI('/resellers/');
                const listElement = document.getElementById('resellers-list');
                listElement.innerHTML = '';

                if (!resellers || resellers.length === 0) {
                    listElement.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;">Nenhum revendedor encontrado.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }

                resellers.forEach((user) => {
                    let limitDisplay = user.client_limit;
                    if (user.role === 'super_admin') limitDisplay = '∞';

                    const userStr = JSON.stringify(user).replace(/'/g, '&#39;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
                    const rowClass = !user.is_active ? 'blocked-row' : '';
                    const toggleButtonText = user.is_active ? '🚫 Bloquear' : '✅ Desbloquear';
                    const toggleButtonClass = user.is_active ? 'active' : 'blocked';

                    listElement.innerHTML += `
                        <tr class="${rowClass}">
                            <td>${user.id}</td>
                            <td>${user.name}</td>
                            <td>${user.email}</td>
                            <td><span class="badge-role-reseller">${user.role}</span></td>
                            <td><strong>${limitDisplay}</strong></td>
                            <td>
                                <button class="btn btn-edit" onclick='openEditModal(${userStr})'>✏️ Editar</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-reset" onclick="resetPassword(${user.id})">🔑 Resetar Senha</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-toggle-active ${toggleButtonClass}" onclick="toggleActive(${user.id}, ${user.is_active})">${toggleButtonText}</button>
                                <button class="btn btn-delete" onclick="deleteReseller(${user.id})">🗑️ Deletar</button>
                            </td>
                        </tr>`;
                });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
            }
        }

        async function loadUsersToPromote() {
            try {
                const promotableUsers = await fetchAPI('/resellers/promotable');
                const selectElement = document.getElementById('user-to-promote');
                selectElement.innerHTML = '<option value="">Selecione um usuário</option>';

                if (promotableUsers && promotableUsers.length > 0) {
                    promotableUsers.forEach((user) => {
                        selectElement.innerHTML += `<option value="${user.id}">${user.name} (${user.email})</option>`;
                    });
                } else {
                    selectElement.innerHTML = '<option value="">Nenhum usuário comum disponível.</option>';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(error);
            }
        }

        document.getElementById('register-form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            const name = document.getElementById('reg-name').value;
            const email = document.getElementById('reg-email').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('reg-password').value;
            const confirmPass = document.getElementById('reg-confirm-password').value;

            if (password !== confirmPass) {
                alert('As senhas não coincidem!');
                return;
            }

            try {
                const newUser = await fetchAPI('/resellers/create-user', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ name, email, password })
                });

                alert(`Usuário "${newUser.name}" cadastrado com sucesso! Agora você pode promovê-lo na lista abaixo.`);
                document.getElementById('register-form').reset();
                await loadUsersToPromote();
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Erro ao cadastrar: ${error.message}`);
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('promote-form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            const userId = document.getElementById('user-to-promote').value;
            const credits = document.getElementById('credits-to-give').value;

            if (!userId) return alert('Selecione um usuário.');
            if (!credits || credits < 1) return alert('Defina uma quantidade válida de limite.');
            if (!confirm(`Promover este usuário a revendedor e definir limite de ${credits}?`)) return;

            try {
                await fetchAPI(`/resellers/promote/${userId}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ credits: parseInt(credits, 10) })
                });

                alert('Sucesso! Usuário promovido e limite definido.');
                location.reload();
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Erro: ${error.message}`);
            }
        });

        function openEditModal(user) {
            document.getElementById('edit-id').value = user.id;
            document.getElementById('edit-name').value = user.name;
            document.getElementById('edit-email').value = user.email;
            document.getElementById('edit-limit').value = user.client_limit;
            document.getElementById('editModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeEditModal() {
            document.getElementById('editModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        window.onclick = function (event) {
            const modal = document.getElementById('editModal');
            if (event.target === modal) {
                closeEditModal();
            }
        };

        document.getElementById('edit-form').addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            const id = document.getElementById('edit-id').value;
            const name = document.getElementById('edit-name').value;
            const email = document.getElementById('edit-email').value;
            const limit = document.getElementById('edit-limit').value;

            try {
                await fetchAPI(`/resellers/${id}`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        name: name,
                        email: email,
                        client_limit: parseInt(limit, 10)
                    })
                });

                alert('Revendedor atualizado com sucesso!');
                closeEditModal();
                location.reload();
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Erro ao atualizar: ${error.message}`);
            }
        });

        async function resetPassword(userId) {
            const newPassword = prompt('Digite a NOVA senha para este usuário (mínimo 6 caracteres):');
            if (!newPassword || newPassword.length < 6) {
                alert('Operação cancelada ou senha inválida.');
                return;
            }
            if (!confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja resetar a senha do usuário ID ${userId}?`)) return;

            try {
                await fetchAPI(`/resellers/${userId}/reset-password`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ new_password: newPassword })
                });
                alert('Senha resetada com sucesso!');
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Erro ao resetar senha: ${error.message}`);
            }
        }

        async function toggleActive(userId, isActive) {
            let reason = null;

            if (isActive) {
                reason = prompt('Digite o motivo do bloqueio (Ex: Falta de pagamento):');
                if (reason === null) return;
                if (reason.trim() === '') reason = 'Motivo não especificado';
                if (!confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja BLOQUEAR o usuário ID ${userId} pelo motivo: "${reason}"?`)) return;
            } else if (!confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja DESBLOQUEAR o acesso do usuário ID ${userId}?`)) {
                return;
            }

            try {
                await fetchAPI(`/resellers/${userId}/toggle-active`, {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ reason: reason })
                });

                const action = isActive ? 'bloqueado' : 'desbloqueado';
                alert(`Usuário ${action} com sucesso!`);
                location.reload();
            } catch (error) {
                const action = isActive ? 'bloquear' : 'desbloquear';
                alert(`Erro ao ${action} usuário: ${error.message}`);
            }
        }

        async function deleteReseller(userId) {
            if (!confirm(`Tem certeza que deseja DELETAR o usuário ID ${userId}? Esta ação é irreversível.`)) return;

            try {
                await fetchAPI(`/resellers/${userId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                alert('Usuário removido com sucesso!');
                location.reload();
            } catch (error) {
                alert(`Erro ao deletar usuário: ${error.message}`);
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
